<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I replaced a $120k bowling center system with $1,600 in ESP32s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 1,600 美元的 ESP32 替代 12 万美元的保龄球馆系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k. The project demonstrates how commodity microcontrollers and open-source software can replace expensive, vendor-locked solutions in real-world venues. It also invites evaluation of trade-offs around reliability, maintenance, and support in commercial deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。此项目展示了商用场景中，普通微控制器与开源软件仍有能力替代昂贵的、厂商绑定的解决方案。亦需权衡可靠性、维护与售后支持等现实挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://raymyers.org/post/zed-creator-calls-spade-a-spade/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig Creator Calls Spade a Spade, Anthropic Blows Smoke</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig 创作者直言不讳，Anthropic 敷衍其词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 761</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outspoken Zig language creator takes a blunt stance on AI safety claims associated with Anthropic, challenging the industry’s framing and transparency. The controversy underscores the tension between ambitious safety promises and practical concerns from developers who build on AI tools. It highlights how independent voices can shape public scrutiny of AI vendors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 语言的创作者对 Anthropic 的 AI 安全说法发表直言，挑战行业的表述与透明度。此事凸显了安全承诺的宏大叙事与开发者对 AI 工具的实际关切之间的张力。独立声音可能影响对 AI 供应商的公众监督。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS: Inaccurate Estimated Billing Data – $1.7 billion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS：估算账单数据不准确，涉额17亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 744</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Web Services disclosed inaccuracies in its estimated billing data, leading to charges totaling about $1.7B across customers. The incident highlights how cloud-cost visibility can fail at scale and underscores the need for robust auditing, refund mechanisms, and better tooling for cost governance in large cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>云成本监控在大规模云环境中暴露出不足，估算账单数据存在不准确之处，涉案金额约17亿美元。此事凸显需要更强的成本可视化、审计与退款流程，并推动改进的成本管理工具与治理方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

Open weights enable researchers to compare methods and stress-test safety features, potentially accelerating progress while requiring thoughtful governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inkling 是 Thinking Machines 的开源权重模型。这体现了对透明、可测试架构的推进。开源权重便于研究者对比方法、测试安全性，但也需要谨慎治理以防滥用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://videocardz.com/newz/lg-monitors-silently-install-software-through-windows-update-without-user-consent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG monitors silently install software through Windows Update without consent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG显示器通过Windows更新在未征得同意的情况下悄悄安装软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG monitors were found to silently install companion software through Windows Update without explicit user consent. The bundled software can run in the background and may raise privacy and security concerns, creating a potential for bloat. Readers should review update offers, remove unwanted components, and consider blocking vendor software when possible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，LG显示器通过Windows Update在未明确同意的情况下安装附带软件。该软件可能后台运行，带来隐私与安全风险，并可能造成系统臃肿。读者应在更新时谨慎勾选、移除不需要的组件，并在可行时阻止厂商软件推送。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.videocardz.com/1/2026/07/LG-MONITORS-MCAFFE.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.videocardz.com/1/2026/07/LG-MONITORS-MCAFFE.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fabiensanglard.net/jurrasic_park_computers/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jurassic Park computers in excruciating detail</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《侏罗纪公园》中的计算机：详尽到极致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece dissects the film&#x27;s on-screen computer systems with an eye for hardware, OS choices, and faux interfaces. It reveals how the prop-makers staged computing to look convincing on 1993-era tech, shaping viewers&#x27; perception of digital authority. The analysis also explains why these UI aesthetics continue to influence cinematic design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文逐步拆解《侏罗纪公园》中的银幕计算机，揭示道具团队如何选用真实硬件与界面语言来营造可信感，并分析这对当代电影界面设计的影响。作者还讨论其对后续影视作品在呈现技术方面的影响力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Alibaba_Qwen/status/2078759124914098291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 828</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 573</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen 3.8 is the latest release in its Qwen family, aiming to improve instruction-following and response quality. The update underscores a continued push by Chinese tech firms to scale capable LLMs for both consumer apps and enterprise use. As with similar releases, safety, guardrails, and domain-specific tuning are likely key focus areas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿里巴巴推出 Qwen 3.8，作为 Qwen 系列的最新版本，力求提升指令遵循能力与输出质量。此举反映中国厂商在大模型领域继续扩张的趋势，覆盖消费者应用与企业场景。与其他厂商的更新一样，安全性、约束机制和领域定制将成为核心关注点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48936426' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/2026/07/16/microsoft-comic-chat-is-now-open-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Comic Chat is now open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软 Comic Chat 现已开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 798</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft released the classic Comic Chat client as open source, reviving a piece of 1990s online chat UI and protocol. The release provides a sandbox for experimentation with vintage software, preserving artifacts and teaching UX evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软将经典的 Comic Chat 客户端开源，重现了 1990 年代的在线聊天界面和协议。此次发布为复古软件的实验、保存与研究提供了机会，也揭示了聊天用户体验的演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Microsoft365-FY26-Consumer-C2-ArtStudio-2749-RGB-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Microsoft365-FY26-Consumer-C2-ArtStudio-2749-RGB-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48930454' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pigeonsandplanes.com/read/music-piracy-what-cd-oink-nine-inch-nails-streaming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The lost joy of music piracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>音乐盗版的逝去乐趣</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 796</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 563</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Streaming turned music into a predictable, licensed product, draining the anarchic joy of early piracy. The piece reflects on how the pirate-era culture created camaraderie and discovery, and asks what we lose when access becomes commodified.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>流媒体让音乐变成可控的授权商品，削弱了早期盗版带来的狂热和社区感。本文回顾那段盗版文化如何塑造了发现与分享的乐趣，并讨论当下更易获取的市场会让我们错过哪些宝贵的文化体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pu3h3rl5/production/61dfa8f019d1a62c96f4a72bfb734ede527e0c90-1920x1080.gif?rect=0,39,1920,1003&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pu3h3rl5/production/61dfa8f019d1a62c96f4a72bfb734ede527e0c90-1920x1080.gif?rect=0,39,1920,1003&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48869989' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danq.me/2026/07/09/your-app-could-have-been-a-webpage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your &#x27;app&#x27; could have been a webpage (so I fixed it for you)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的“应用”其实可以是网页版本——我把它改成网页了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48869989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 795</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48869989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 474</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical take on web vs native apps: many projects can be delivered as a robust webpage, reducing maintenance and improving accessibility. The post shows concrete steps to flatten typical app functionality into a responsive web UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谈及网页与原生应用的现实取舍：很多项目其实可以用强大的网页版本来实现，降低维护成本并提升可访问性。文中给出将常见应用功能转换成响应式网页界面的具体做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://danq.me/_q23-og/29641/0.png' data-full='https://danq.me/_q23-og/29641/0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48901569' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.supercarblondie.com/japan-recovers-up-to-90-of-lithium-from-used-ev-batteries/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan develops a method to recover up to 90% of lithium from used EV batteries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本开发可从废旧新能源汽车电池回收高达90%的锂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 733</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan has demonstrated a process capable of recovering up to 90% of lithium from spent EV batteries, boosting recycling efficiency and extending battery supply resilience. The advance could lower material costs and lessen environmental impact by reducing mining and refining needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本展示了一种可从废旧电动车电池中回收锂高达90%的工艺，显著提升回收效率并加强电池材料保障。此举可能降低成本、减轻采矿与精炼带来的环境压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tech.supercarblondie.com/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=675,quality=80,format=webp,fit=cover/wp-content/uploads/Japan-lithium-featured.webp' data-full='https://tech.supercarblondie.com/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=675,quality=80,format=webp,fit=cover/wp-content/uploads/Japan-lithium-featured.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48963879' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://workshop.cjpais.com/projects/transcribe-cpp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Transcribe.cpp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transcribe.cpp 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48963879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 731</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48963879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Transcribe.cpp presents a compact C++ project that demonstrates a speech-to-text workflow. It walks through building a lightweight transcription pipeline and discusses latency versus accuracy trade-offs, including how to structure streaming versus batch transcription.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Transcribe.cpp 展示了一个精简的 C++ 转写实现，展示了将音频转换为文本的基本流程，并讨论了延迟与准确率之间的权衡，包含流式与批量转写的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48919363' data-tags='Health,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://academic.oup.com/sleep/article/47/1/zsad253/7280269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sleep regularity is a stronger predictor of mortality risk than sleep duration (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>睡眠规律性比睡眠时长更能预测死亡风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research suggests that how regularly you sleep predicts mortality risk more strongly than total sleep duration. Using long-term wearable data, the study links consistent sleep timing with better health outcomes, independent of hours slept. The finding shifts emphasis toward regular schedules as a health strategy and dovetails with consumer wellness tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明，睡眠时间的规律性与死亡风险的相关性比总睡眠时长更强。通过长期可穿戴设备数据，研究发现稳定的睡眠时序与更好的健康结果相关，且与实际睡眠时长无关。该发现将健康策略的重点从睡眠时长转向规律作息，并与可穿戴健康科技的发展相吻合。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48936584' data-tags='Design,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mixfont.com/experiments/decoy-font' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decoy Font</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decoy 字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decoy Font is an experimental typeface designed to confuse automated readers and OCR systems by mapping glyphs in unusual ways. It highlights typography’s impact on AI text detectors and reveals current recognition limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decoy 字体是一种实验性字体，通过以异常的方式映射字形来混淆自动识别系统与 OCR。它揭示排版对 AI 文本检测的影响与当前识别能力的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.mixfont.com/assets/20260715-083635-decoy-font-og-ctfx685m.webp' data-full='https://static.mixfont.com/assets/20260715-083635-decoy-font-og-ctfx685m.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48883275' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code sends 33k tokens before reading the prompt; OpenCode sends 7k</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 在读取提示前消耗 3.3 万令牌；OpenCode 仅约 7 千令牌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 689</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens. The gap highlights how different toolchains incur context overhead that affects latency, cost, and developer workflows in AI-assisted coding. This emphasizes the importance of managing token budgets when chaining multiple AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。这一差距揭示了不同工具链在上下文开销上的差异，进而影响 AI 辅助编码的延迟、成本和工作流。强调在串联多家 AI 服务时对令牌预算进行管理的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48910545' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-27b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bonsai 27B: A 27B-Class model that runs on a phone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bonsai 27B：能在手机上运行的27B级模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bonsai 27B project demonstrates a 27-billion-parameter model that can run directly on a smartphone, signaling a practical edge-AI milestone. Achieving on-device inference requires aggressive optimization (pruning, quantization, efficient runtimes) to fit memory and power constraints, but it promises lower latency and stronger privacy. This shift hints at a future where powerful AI can operate without constant cloud access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bonsai 27B 展示了一个27B参数级模型能够直接在手机上运行，标志着边缘AI的一个实际里程碑。实现本地推理需要对模型进行大幅优化（裁剪、量化、高效运行时），以适应内存与电池限制，但可带来更低的延迟和更强的隐私保护。这预示着强大AI有望在不依赖云端的情况下运行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/697a3312d33c2cc715ec3899/69961847735f3ec32aafc18e_prism-logo.svg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/697a3312d33c2cc715ec3899/69961847735f3ec32aafc18e_prism-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48954851' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maurycyz.com/projects/bad_jpeg/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Regressive JPEGs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归式 JPEG 图像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48954851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48954851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative look at JPEG compression artifacts and how regressive techniques alter image quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨 JPEG 压缩中的伪影，以及回归式方法如何改变或揭示这种压缩行为。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lyrebirddreaming.com/post/the-graph-that-should-be-front-page-news' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A graph that should be front-page news</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>应该登上头条的图表</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48888331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 671</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48888331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data viz claim that a particular graph reveals a trend with broad implications, arguing it deserves widespread attention beyond niche circles. The post emphasizes the data’s relevance for tech policy, business strategy, or public understanding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张图表揭示的趋势具有广泛影响，作者认为应成为主流话题而非小众知识。该图强调数据对科技政策、商业策略或公众认知的相关性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48933419' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/07/15/sony-deletes-a-bunch-more-movies-from-the-accounts-of-people-who-bought-them/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony deletes more movies from the accounts of people who ‘bought’ them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼再次从购买者账户中删除多部电影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 661</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 410</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony has removed additional titles from users’ digital libraries after purchase, prompting questions about ownership, DRM, and what it means to &#x27;own&#x27; digital media. The incident spotlights ongoing tensions in rights, licensing, and user trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼再次从已购买用户的数字库中删除多部电影，引发关于数字所有权、DRM 与所有权概念的讨论。此事凸显版权、许可与用户信任之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ayush.digital/blog/the-memory-heist' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I tricked Claude into leaking your deepest, darkest secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何骗过 Claude 让它泄露你最深的秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 647</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A crafted prompt demonstrates a vulnerability in Claude&#x27;s handling of sensitive data, revealing how advanced LLMs can leak information under certain prompts. The post underscores the data-privacy risk and the importance of guardrails, data governance, and model hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过巧妙的提示演示，揭示 Claude 在特定输入下可能泄露敏感信息的风险，凸显数据隐私与模型防护的重要性。文章提醒开发者加强 guardrails、数据治理和对模型的安全强化。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/math/comments/1uxj3cy/after_openais_cdc_proof_announcement_gpt56_used_a/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 used a prompt to close a 30-year gap in convex optimization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 用提示弥补凸优化三十年难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48957779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 580</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48957779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A prompt reportedly closed a 30-year gap in convex optimization after a CDC-style proof announcement. If verified, it demonstrates how carefully crafted prompts can guide AI to tackle deep mathematical problems and produce testable steps. The claim invites scrutiny of reproducibility and the proper role of AI in formal proofs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，GPT-5.6 通过一个提示，解决了凸优化领域长达30年的未解难题，这一成果在 CDC 风格证明公告后出现。若属实，表明大型语言模型在帮助人类数学家进行问题表述和证据推导方面具有潜在价值，同时也需要对可重复性与独立验证保持警惕。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/3a023b95-66c3-41e1-b0ce-df752a499541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX bond worth 10% less than issue price – heading for junk bond status</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 债券发行价下跌 10%：有望走向垃圾债评级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48920181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48920181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX bonds trade roughly 10% below issue price, signaling waning investor demand or rising perceived risk. If the debt slides toward junk status, SpaceX could face higher refinancing costs and tighter liquidity windows as macro headwinds bite tech funding. This serves as a reminder that even highly popular tech issuers are not immune to debt-market sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 债券交易价格约较发行价低约10%，反映市场对其风险的重新定价或需求走弱。若债务评级进一步恶化，再融资成本将上升，流动性可能收紧，凸显当前宏观环境对科技股相关债务的冲击。此事再次印证即使是备受关注的科技发行也会受债市情绪影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://get-inscribe.com/blog/apple-speech-api-benchmark.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s new SpeechAnalyzer API, benchmarked against Whisper and its predecessor</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s new SpeechAnalyzer API is benchmarked against Whisper and Apple&#x27;s own older speech API. The results highlight on-device performance, latency, and accuracy tradeoffs, offering insights into when to choose cloud vs. on-device processing for voice apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果新推出的 SpeechAnalyzer API 经过与 Whisper 及前代 API 的对比基准测试，凸显了设备端与云端推理的权衡。评测聚焦准确率、延迟和噪声鲁棒性，为构建本地化语音应用提供实用参考。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://petapixel.com/2026/07/16/mayor-mamdani-says-landlords-cant-secretly-use-ai-images-to-advertise-properties/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC may require landlords and realtors to disclose the use of AI in listings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市可能要求房东披露在房源信息中使用 AI 的情况</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City is considering regulations to require landlords and real estate agents to disclose the use of AI-generated imagery in listings. The move aims to curb deceptive advertising and increase transparency in online property listings, with implications for proptech platforms and compliance burdens.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市正考虑立法，要求房东和中介披露房源照片中是否使用了 AI 生成的图像。此举旨在提升在线房源广告的透明度，影响房地产科技平台的合规成本与运营方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://petapixel.com/assets/uploads/2026/07/mamdani-copy.jpg' data-full='https://petapixel.com/assets/uploads/2026/07/mamdani-copy.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48952880' data-tags='AI,Health,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://localnewsmatters.org/2026/07/15/kaiser-nurses-say-ai-workplace-surveillance-are-making-their-jobs-and-patient-care-worse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kaiser nurses say AI, surveillance are making their jobs and patient care worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>凯撒医疗系统护士称 AI 与监控让工作和病人护理变得更糟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Frontline nurses report that AI decision-support and surveillance systems add administrative burden and impair patient interaction, potentially harming care quality. The piece underscores calls for better governance, transparency, and careful deployment of automation in healthcare.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一线护士表示人工智能决策支持和工作场所监控增加了工作负担，影响与病人互动，可能损及护理质量。文章强调需要更好的治理、透明度，以及在医疗领域更谨慎地部署自动化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/localnewsmatters.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/LNM-20260713-MATTERSKAISER-01.jpg?fit=2000%2C1333&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/localnewsmatters.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/LNM-20260713-MATTERSKAISER-01.jpg?fit=2000%2C1333&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48903777' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/eu-digital-identity-wallet/av-doc-technical-specification/discussions/19' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European &quot;age verification&quot; &quot;app&quot; forcing everyone to use Android or iOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲的年龄验证应用强制使用 Android 或 iOS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 551</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European age-verification discussions reveal a regulatory push that effectively forces users onto mobile platforms, raising concerns about privacy, interoperability, and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于欧洲年龄验证应用的讨论揭示了一个监管推动，实质上强制用户依赖 Android 或 iOS，带来隐私、互操作性与可访问性方面的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/763f5184b61bd0127a10c1cddee3e05d6f53474eab84ed816cd0a86b9be9267a/eu-digital-identity-wallet/av-doc-technical-specification/discussions/19' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/763f5184b61bd0127a10c1cddee3e05d6f53474eab84ed816cd0a86b9be9267a/eu-digital-identity-wallet/av-doc-technical-specification/discussions/19' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48911793' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/moonshine-ai/moonshine/tree/main/micro' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speech Recognition and TTS in less than 500kb</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不到500KB的語音識別與文字轉語音</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48911793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48911793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ultra-low-footprint speech recognition and TTS can run in under 500KB, enabling offline voice interfaces on microcontrollers and other constrained devices. The project highlights design choices that trade off accuracy for size, with potential uses in privacy-preserving, edge AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>極低資源的語音識別與文字轉語音能在不足500KB內運行，使微控制器和受限設備具備離線語音輸入/輸出能力。該項目展示了為了縮小尺寸而進行的設計取捨，對注重隱私的邊緣AI場景尤具吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/867866058/0fd49719-61f4-4a3d-bb6f-eda467526a0c' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/867866058/0fd49719-61f4-4a3d-bb6f-eda467526a0c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://19thnews.org/2026/07/noaa-climate-data-website/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Former NOAA employees built Climate.us to preserve climate data and resources</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48897945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48897945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project by former NOAA staff to archive climate data and related resources, addressing data accessibility and preservation challenges as data grows and policies evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由前 NOAA 员工创建的 Climate.us 项目，旨在存档气候数据及相关资源，解决数据日益增长与政策变化带来的获取与长期保存挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scottwillsey.com/building-and-shipping-mac-and-ios-apps-without-ever-opening-xcode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building and shipping Mac and iOS apps without opening Xcode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需打开 Xcode 就能构建与发布 Mac 与 iOS 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a workflow to build and ship Mac and iOS apps without ever opening Xcode, relying on CLI tools and automation. It highlights how signing, packaging, and deployment can be automated in CI/CD, reducing GUI-focused work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了一条无需打开 Xcode 就能构建和发布 Mac 与 iOS 应用的工作流，依赖命令行工具与自动化。强调在 CI/CD 中自动化签名、打包与部署，减少对 GUI 的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scottwillsey.com/_astro/saw111.Cprn7ULf_ZB6aHy.webp' data-full='https://scottwillsey.com/_astro/saw111.Cprn7ULf_ZB6aHy.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/7/13/the-tower-keeps-rising/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Tower Keeps Rising</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48909785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 541</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48909785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech systems keep piling on layers and complexity as projects scale, increasing maintenance overhead and slowing developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着项目规模扩大，技术系统不断叠加层级与复杂性，导致维护成本上升、开发者体验受挫。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-07-13-the-tower-keeps-rising-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-07-13-the-tower-keeps-rising-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48947560' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cy4kdd1e0ejo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>First atmosphere found on Earth-like planet in habitable zone of distant star</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在遥远恒星的宜居带首次发现地球样行星的大气</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers detected atmospheric signals on an Earth-like planet in a distant star’s habitable zone, marking a milestone in characterizing rocky worlds beyond our solar system. The finding invigorates plans for future telescopes to search for biosignatures and assess habitability on exoplanets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家在遥远恒星的宜居带内探测到地球样行星的大气信号，标志着对系外岩石行星表征的重大进展。此发现将推动未来望远镜在星际尺度上寻找生物征兆并评估宜居性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2cd8/live/f3f60290-81d1-11f1-926f-c90d1bcfbc84.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2cd8/live/f3f60290-81d1-11f1-926f-c90d1bcfbc84.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48908178' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.artfish.ai/p/offloading-thinking-to-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are we offloading too much of our thinking to AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们是不是把太多思考交给 AI？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 474</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>We&#x27;re increasingly offloading thinking to AI, but overreliance risks dulling critical thinking, misframing problems, and eroding human agency. The piece calls for balancing automation with human judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我们越来越多地把思考交给 AI，文章认为过度依赖会削弱批判性思维、使问题界定偏离实际，并侵蚀个人主动性。应在自动化与人类判断之间保持平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!NTK9!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb548edd-f827-4d19-aa23-3bd68fdc980c_1456x1092.webp' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!NTK9!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb548edd-f827-4d19-aa23-3bd68fdc980c_1456x1092.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48892512' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/a_green_being/status/2076598897779020159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok uploaded my user directory to xAI&#x27;s servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 将我的用户目录上传至 xAI 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports that Grok uploaded their entire user directory to xAI&#x27;s servers, exposing sensitive data and raising privacy concerns around AI tooling. The incident highlights the data-guardrails gap in developer workflows and the need for stricter data handling and disclosure practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户声称 Grok 将其整个用户目录上传至 xAI 的服务器，暴露敏感数据并引发对 AI 工具中数据隐私的担忧。此事暴露了开发工作流中对数据保护的不足，强调需要更严格的数据处理与披露规范。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNGOF9QXwAAU_jp.png:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNGOF9QXwAAU_jp.png:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48933149' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rtfeldman.com/rust-to-zig' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Our Rust-to-Zig Rewrite Is Going</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们的 Rust 转 Zig 重写进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 507</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Progress update on rewriting a Rust codebase in Zig, detailing early results, design choices, and remaining hurdles. It outlines potential gains in memory safety, binary size, and cross-platform portability while acknowledging migration complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文更新了将 Rust 代码库改写为 Zig 的进展，介绍关键设计取舍、初步成果与尚待解决的挑战。讨论了在内存安全、二进制大小和跨平台可移植性方面的潜在收益，同时也强调迁移的复杂性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geohot.github.io//blog/jekyll/update/2026/07/12/i-love-llms.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I love LLMs, I hate hype</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我爱大语言模型，也讨厌炒作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 318</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A measured take on LLMs, acknowledging their capabilities while warning against hype, and urging careful evaluation, realistic expectations, and responsible deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对大语言模型保持审慎态度，既肯定其能力，又批评炒作，倡导理性评估、务实期望和负责任的落地应用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48947825' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stateofopensource.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The state of open source AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla：开源 AI 的现状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-source AI platforms are maturing, with new models, licensing debates, and governance challenges shaping the ecosystem. The piece benchmarks how open tooling stacks up against proprietary options and why safety, transparency, and community norms matter for long-term viability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源 AI 正在走向成熟，新的模型、许可与治理挑战共同塑造生态。相较于商业垄断，开放工具链在安全性、透明度和社区规范方面的表现将决定其长期可用性与影响力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stateofopensource.ai/assets/img/moz-logo.png' data-full='https://stateofopensource.ai/assets/img/moz-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/finance/stripe-advent-offer-buy-paypal-more-than-53-billion-sources-say-2026-07-15/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stripe and Advent have made a joint offer to acquire PayPal – sources</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stripe 与 Advent 联合收购 PayPal</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 472</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stripe and Advent reportedly submitted a joint bid to buy PayPal for over $53 billion, signaling a consolidation push in payments infrastructure. The proposed deal would reshape how merchants access payments, wallets, and risk management services at platform scale. If completed, it could set a precedent for fintech M&amp;A and platform-powered finance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据悉，Stripe 与 Advent 提出联合收购 PayPal 的报价，金额超过 530 亿美元，表明支付基础设施领域正迎来整合。若成行，交易将重塑商家获取支付、钱包与风控服务的方式，并推动平台化金融的发展。完成交易还可能为金融科技并购树立新基准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 469</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 411</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The LAPD chose not to renew its contract with surveillance company Flock, letting it expire amid serious concerns over civil liberties and privacy. The move signals growing scrutiny of AI-powered facial recognition tech and could influence how cities vet surveillance vendors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>洛杉矶警察局选择不续签与监控公司 Flock 的合约，让其到期。此举是在对公民自由与隐私的日益严格审查背景下作出的，或将影响城市在监督性监控技术与供应商方面的选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipweinberger.com/articles/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What I learned selling 2,500 MIDI recorders: Hardware is not so hard</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 447</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops. Expect practical notes on prototyping, cost control, manufacturing, and post-sale support for indie hardware projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。文章提供从原型、成本控制到制造与售后支持的实用要点，适合独立硬件创业者借鉴。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mindgard.ai/blog/cursor-0day-when-full-disclosure-becomes-the-only-protection-left' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cursor 0day: When Full Disclosure Becomes the Only Protection Left</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cursor 0day：当完全披露成为唯一的保护时</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece weighs the ethics and practicality of full disclosure for zero-day vulnerabilities, arguing that disclosure can become the only robust defense when other protections fail. It contrasts responsible disclosure with more aggressive release approaches and calls for rethinking incentives and defense layers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨零日漏洞的完全披露在道德与实务上的权衡，认为在其他防护失效时披露可能成为唯一的可靠防线。对比负责任披露与更激进的公开方式，呼吁重新思考激励机制与多层防御。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/a_green_being/status/2076598897779020159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok CLI uploaded the whole home directory to GCS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok CLI 将整个 home 目录上传至 GCS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 432</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 403</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok CLI uploaded the entire home directory to Google Cloud Storage, revealing a potential misconfiguration or unsafe behavior in a developer tool. The incident underscores the importance of sane defaults, local data handling, and thorough review of CLI tool data flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok CLI 将整个 home 目录上传至 GCS，暴露了开发工具中的潜在配置错误或不安全行为。此事件强调了默认设置的安全性、本地数据处理以及对 CLI 工具数据流的全面审查的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNGOF9QXwAAU_jp.png:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNGOF9QXwAAU_jp.png:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48959090' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.benlandautaylor.com/p/if-you-build-it-they-will-come' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If You Build It, They Will Come</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>若你建好，他们就会来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48959090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48959090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Challenging the adage &#x27;If you build it, they will come,&#x27; the piece argues that building without a clear distribution and engagement plan often fails. It emphasizes the need for user research, onboarding, and active community-building to ensure product adoption. Without these, even great software may struggle to find its audience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章挑战‘建好就有人来’的格言，认为若没有清晰的推广与用户获取计划，产品往往难以成功。强调需要用户研究、良好引导和积极的社区建设来推动采用。否则再优秀的软件也可能找不到受众。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!L0xO!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbb128a68-b1f1-44c9-8d42-a01d689ca8c8_1554x908.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!L0xO!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbb128a68-b1f1-44c9-8d42-a01d689ca8c8_1554x908.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48956924' data-tags='Design,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://velvetshark.com/ai-company-logos-that-look-like-buttholes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why do AI company logos look like buttholes? (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI公司标志为何看起来像肛门？（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 430</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI company logos are trending toward circular motifs and negative space that resemble a certain anatomical shape. It highlights the tension between simple design and cultural signaling in AI branding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI公司标志日渐采用圆形和空白区来营造简约风格，却意外产生具争议性的联想。文章探讨这种极简设计如何影响品牌记忆点与形象认知，以及在AI品牌中需要权衡的文化信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://velvetshark.com/images/ai-company-logos-that-look-like-buttholes/why-do-AI-company-logos-look-like-buttholes.png' data-full='https://velvetshark.com/images/ai-company-logos-that-look-like-buttholes/why-do-AI-company-logos-look-like-buttholes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48884853' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scrapfly.dev/posts/browser-math-os-fingerprint/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Since Chromium 148, Math.tanh is now fingerprintable to link underlying OS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自 Chromium 148 版本起，Math.tanh 可用于指纹识别并关联底层操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 426</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chromium 148 introduces a fingerprinting vector based on Math.tanh that can link a browser fingerprint to the underlying OS. This creates a new privacy risk by allowing device-level identity to be correlated with browser signals, potentially undermining anonymity. The finding underscores the need for stronger anti-fingerprinting defenses in browsers and better OS-level mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chromium 148 引入了一种基于 Math.tanh 的指纹识别向量，能够将浏览器指纹与底层操作系统关联起来。这一隐私风险使设备级身份识别和浏览器指纹之间形成关联，削弱匿名性。相关发现强调需要加强浏览器的防指纹跟踪能力以及操作系统层面的缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scrapfly.dev/img/og-default.png' data-full='https://scrapfly.dev/img/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48905028' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/28058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex starts encrypting sub-agent prompts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 开始对子代理提示进行加密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI Codex now encrypts prompts used by sub-agents to protect sensitive instructions across agent calls. The change reduces prompt leakage and improves security for multi-agent workflows, though it may complicate debugging and require key management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI Codex 现对子代理所使用的提示进行加密，以在代理调用之间保护敏感指令。此举降低了提示泄露风险，提升多代理工作流的安全性，但可能增加调试难度与密钥管理负担。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d433c94f61a2fd72e152a08799aac44fa427c722df72ed02f0671fcfe2df18b8/openai/codex/issues/28058' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d433c94f61a2fd72e152a08799aac44fa427c722df72ed02f0671fcfe2df18b8/openai/codex/issues/28058' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48956949' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://data.stackexchange.com/stackoverflow/query/1953768#graph' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What AI did to stackoverflow in a graph</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 对 Stack Overflow 的影响：以图展示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 506</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data visualization chronicles how AI involvement has reshaped Stack Overflow, showing shifts in question types, answer volumes, and answer quality. The graph highlights AI&#x27;s dual role as amplifier of productivity and potential source of noise, prompting platform governance discussions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张数据可视化图揭示人工智能参与对 Stack Overflow 的影响：提问类型、回答数量和质量的变化。图表显示 AI 既提升生产力又可能带来噪音，促使对社区治理与质量控制的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jul/16/kimi-k3/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3, and what we can still learn from the pelican benchmark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3 与 Pelican 基准测试中的可学到的教训</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisits Kimi K3 alongside the Pelican benchmark to draw practical lessons about benchmarking practice. It emphasizes reproducibility, transparent metrics, and which signals actually matter to real-world performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>重新审视 Kimi K3 与 Pelican 基准测试，提炼出对基准实践的实用见解。强调可重复性、指标透明度，以及对实际性能有用的关键信号。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/kimi-3-pelican.jpg' data-full='https://static.simonwillison.net/static/2026/kimi-3-pelican.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48834309' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nebusec.ai/research/ionstack-part-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GhostLock, a stack-UAF that has existed in all Linux distributions for 15 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GhostLock：已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在 15 年的栈上使用后释放漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GhostLock is a stack-use-after-free vulnerability that has persisted across every Linux distribution for 15 years. Its ubiquity makes patching difficult and raises questions about how many systems remain unpatched, underscoring the need for systemic mitigations and kernel-level hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GhostLock 是一个栈上使用后释放漏洞，已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在长达 15 年。问题的广泛性使修补工作复杂且难以全面覆盖，凸显对内核层面对策和系统级强化的迫切需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nebusec.ai/_astro/social-preview.BvQ1tugL.png' data-full='https://nebusec.ai/_astro/social-preview.BvQ1tugL.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48909424' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://marco-nett.de/blog/measuring-input-latency-on-linux-x11-vs-wayland-vrr-dxvk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Measuring Input Latency on Linux: X11 vs. Wayland, VRR, and DXVK</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Linux 上测量输入延迟：X11 与 Wayland、VRR 与 DXVK 的比较</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48909424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48909424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 286</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An empirical study measuring input latency on Linux across X11 and Wayland, with VRR and DXVK in the mix. It establishes a framework for end-to-end latency testing in gaming workloads and highlights how the choice of display server and graphics stack can shift responsiveness. The takeaway for developers is to prefer Wayland for lower latency in gaming scenarios and to be mindful of DXVK overhead depending on the workload.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于对 Linux 输入延迟的对比测量，覆盖 X11、Wayland、VRR 与 DXVK 的组合。研究建立了端到端延迟的测试框架，并揭示了显示服务器和显卡驱动栈的选择对响应速度的影响。结论是游戏场景下 Wayland 往往能带来更低的延迟，同时要关注 DXVK 路径的额外开销与 VRR 带来的抖动收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://marco-nett.de/blog/measuring-input-latency-on-linux-x11-vs-wayland-vrr-dxvk/index.png' data-full='https://marco-nett.de/blog/measuring-input-latency-on-linux-x11-vs-wayland-vrr-dxvk/index.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/1b8c9d52-88a9-426b-ba47-f1811f859166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple targets dozens of OpenAI employees with legal letters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果致函多名 OpenAI 员工</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 352</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple sent legal letters to a number of OpenAI employees, touching IP and contractual concerns tied to AI tooling usage. The move signals intensified corporate scrutiny over AI research and the boundaries of innovation, IP, and employment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果就 AI 工具使用相关的知识产权与合约事宜，对多名 OpenAI 员工发出法律函。此举凸显企业对 AI 研究的更强监管，以及创新、知识产权与雇佣关系之间的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fimages.ft.com%2Fv3%2Fimage%2Fraw%2Fhttps%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2Feb588ccd-457b-4974-bfab-ca7ca8563612.jpg%3Fsource%3Dnext-barrier-page%26fit%3Dscale-down%26quality%3Dhighest%26width%3D700%26dpr%3D1?source=next-barrier-page' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fimages.ft.com%2Fv3%2Fimage%2Fraw%2Fhttps%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2Feb588ccd-457b-4974-bfab-ca7ca8563612.jpg%3Fsource%3Dnext-barrier-page%26fit%3Dscale-down%26quality%3Dhighest%26width%3D700%26dpr%3D1?source=next-barrier-page' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48896286' data-tags='Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://superdario.pawb.de' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Super Dario</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Super Dario</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
