<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.blender.org/download/releases/5-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blender 5.2 LTS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Blender 5.2 LTS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48911021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48911021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blender 5.2 LTS marks the newest long-term support release, prioritizing stability for studios and creators who rely on consistent updates. It brings refinements across rendering, animation tooling, and the Python API, with ongoing bug fixes and security updates planned for years.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Blender 5.2 LTS 发布，面向需要长期稳定更新的工作室与创作者。该版本在渲染、动画工具与 Python API 等方面带来改进，并承诺多年的错误修复与安全更新。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48890959' data-tags='Education,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jivx.com/densha' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A voxel Tokyo in real Japan time – ride the Yamanote line and study Japanese</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>实时像素化的东京：乘坐山手线学习日语的沉浸式体验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48890959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48890959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A voxel-based visualization of Tokyo runs in real time, letting users ride the Yamanote Line and study Japanese in an immersive urban simulation. The project demonstrates how realtime, data-rich simulations can support language learning and cultural immersion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个基于体素的东京实景地图以实时方式呈现，让用户乘坐山手线并学习日语，提供沉浸式的城市仿真体验。该项目展示了实时、数据驱动的模拟如何支持语言学习和文化沉浸。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48936451' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/products/gemini-notebook/notebooklm-gemini-notebook/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NotebookLM is now Gemini Notebook</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google rebrands NotebookLM as Gemini Notebook and ties it more closely to the Gemini AI ecosystem, signaling deeper integration for note-taking, research, and AI-assisted idea capture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 将 NotebookLM 重命名为 Gemini Notebook，并与 Gemini AI 生态系统更紧密地整合，强化笔记、研究与 AI 辅助记录的能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48925271' data-tags='Career,AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://turntrout.com/why-i-left-google-deepmind' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I Left Google DeepMind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何我离开 Google DeepMind</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48925271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48925271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former DeepMind engineer explains leaving due to misalignment between research culture and product timelines, plus governance and compensation frictions. The piece offers candid insight into career choices in AI labs and the tensions between scientific ambition and commercial pragmatism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前 DeepMind 工程师讲述离职原因，强调研究文化与产品落地的错位、治理与激励机制的挑战。文章揭示 AI 实验室里科研野心与商业化需求之间的张力，以及个人在职业选择时需权衡的因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.turntrout.com/static/images/card_images/why-i-left-google-deepmind.jpg' data-full='https://assets.turntrout.com/static/images/card_images/why-i-left-google-deepmind.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48914644' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vpd.ca/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vancouver PD website features Quick Escape button that wipes itself from history</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>温哥华警察局网站设有“快速逃离”按钮，可从历史记录中自行移除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48914644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48914644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Vancouver PD site includes a Quick Escape feature that clears itself from the browser history, intended for users in sensitive situations. This raises questions about privacy-by-design on public portals and how thoroughly such data is erased across logs and devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该网站提供的“快速逃离”按钮旨在保护处于敏感情境的用户隐私，但也引发了对公门户隐私设计的讨论：实际能清除哪些数据、还有哪些日志会被保留。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vpd.ca/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/logo-vpd-white-270x318-1.png' data-full='https://vpd.ca/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/logo-vpd-white-270x318-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48928135' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mort.coffee/home/sqlite-editions/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SQLite should have (Rust-style) editions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQLite 应该有（像 Rust 那样的） Edition</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48928135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48928135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SQLite would benefit from edition-style feature versions to manage incompatible changes and new capabilities. Editions could preserve stable behavior while enabling newer features to be adopted gradually.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者提出让 SQLite 引入类似 Rust 版本的 Edition，以在兼容性变更和新功能之间提供清晰的界限。通过 Editions，用户可在保持稳定行为的同时，逐步引入更先进的功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mort.coffee/_/imgs/rss.svg' data-full='https://mort.coffee/_/imgs/rss.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48897878' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.whois.com/whois/t.me' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Telegram&#x27;s t.me domain has been suspended</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Telegram 的 t.me 短链域名已被暂停</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48897878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48897878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Telegram’s short-link domain t.me has been suspended, breaking many inbound and outbound redirects. The disruption highlights how external domain control can affect critical communication channels and the importance of redundancy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Telegram 的 t.me 短链域名已被暂停，导致大量链接跳转失效。此举凸显外部域名控制对关键通信渠道的影响，以及建立冗余的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.whois.com/images/og.png' data-full='https://www.whois.com/images/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48964185' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ludic.mataroa.blog/blog/ai-mania-is-eviscerating-global-decision-making/#fnref:3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Mania Is Eviscerating Global Decision-Making</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 热潮正在侵蚀全球决策</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical reflection on how hype around AI is shaping policy and governance, potentially eroding thoughtful deliberation. The piece warns that urgency and novelty can override rigorous analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇评论指出，AI 热潮正在影响政策与治理，可能削弱深思熟虑的决策过程。文章警示，紧迫感与新奇性可能压过严格分析。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48854168' data-tags='Politics,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/30/the-father-of-the-internet-is-finally-retiring/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vint Cerf, “father of the Internet”, is retiring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网之父 Vint Cerf 将退休</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48854168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48854168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vint Cerf, a founding figure of the Internet and a long-time advocate for open standards, is stepping away from public roles. His retirement signals a generational shift in Internet governance as new platforms and threats emerge. Cerf’s career underscores the enduring link between protocol design, policy, and global connectivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>互联网之父、长期倡导开放标准的 Vint Cerf 将从公开岗位退下。此举标志着互联网治理的世代转变，面对新平台与新威胁，行业将迎来新的领导力。 Cerf 的职业生涯强调了协议设计、政策制定与全球互联的持久联系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/vint-cerf-GettyImages-454796419.jpg?resize=1200,723' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/05/vint-cerf-GettyImages-454796419.jpg?resize=1200,723' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48897991' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neow.in/cWsyMTV3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung Health app threatens data deletion if users opt out AI training</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星 Health 应用若用户不参与 AI 训练将删除其数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48897991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48897991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung Health policy reportedly deletes user data if they opt out of AI training. The move raises concerns about user consent, data ownership, and the limits of opt-out choices in AI-enabled services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三星健康应用据称若用户不参与 AI 训练就删除其数据。这一政策引发对用户同意、数据所有权以及 AI 服务中隐私权边界的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2026/07/1783952968_samsung-health_story.webp' data-full='https://cdn.neowin.com/news/images/uploaded/2026/07/1783952968_samsung-health_story.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48908214' data-tags='Hardware,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/07/im-a-usb-c-maximalist/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I&#x27;m a USB-C Maximalist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我是 USB-C 极致主义者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 437</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates USB-C as a universal port, stressing interoperability, future-proofing, and regulatory momentum. The piece also considers practical caveats for users and developers adopting USB-C-centric ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张 USB-C 作为通用接口，强调互操作性、前瞻性与监管推动。同时讨论采用以 USB-C 为核心生态时，用户与开发者应关注的实际注意事项。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48887753' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Opinion piece questions what happened to DOGE&#x27;s records, using it as a lens to critique how government data and efficiency claims are documented. It argues that record-keeping and FOIA-era transparency are essential for accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章质疑 DOGE 的记录去向，并借此批评政府数据与效率声称的透明度。作者强调记录保存与信息自由法案时代的问责性对政府治理的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48965850' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/33972/files' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI reduces Codex Model Context Size from 372k to 272k</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的上下文大小从 372k 减少到 272k</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965850' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts. Developers may need to restructure inputs or chunk code to preserve context, potentially affecting large-scale code generation tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。开发者可能需要重新组织输入或将代码拆分成块以维持上下文，进而影响大规模代码生成的任务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/244841968?s=400&amp;v=4' data-full='https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/244841968?s=400&amp;v=4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://floooh.github.io/tiny8bit-preview/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tiny Emulators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>极小型仿真器预览</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Preview showcases ultra-small 8-bit emulators, designed for web and embedded contexts with minimal code. The project aims to deliver authentic retro computing experiences on tiny runtimes, enabling hobbyists to run classic software on constrained devices. It demonstrates how compact emulation can broaden access to retro computing in modern tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该预览展示了极小型的 8 位仿真器，代码量极小，适合在网页或嵌入式环境中运行。项目旨在让在受限设备上也能体验经典的“复古计算”，为爱好者提供运行老软件与工具的可能。该工作体现了极小体量仿真在现代工具链中的应用潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/S-P-downgrades-Oracle-to-BBB-only-one-notch-above-junk-level-11363472.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>S&amp;P Global has lowered Oracle’s creditworthiness from BBB to BBB-</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>标普全球将 Oracle 的信用评级从 BBB 下调至 BBB-</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48909768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48909768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 362</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>S&amp;P Global cut Oracle&#x27;s credit rating by one notch to BBB-. The downgrade signals concerns about Oracle&#x27;s leverage and cash flow as it plows money into cloud growth, potentially raising borrowing costs and narrowing financing options. It reflects investor caution toward large software and cloud players in a higher-rate environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>标普全球将甲骨文的信用等级下调一个档位至 BBB-，显现出对其杠杆、现金流及云增長路径的担忧。随着公司继续大手笔投资云业务，这一评级调整可能推高融资成本并收窄融资选择。这个举动也彰显了在利率上升周期中，对大型软件与云厂商的谨慎情绪。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://heise.cloudimg.io/bound/1200x1200/q85.png-lossy-85.webp-lossy-85.foil1/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/1/1/6/1/8/2/shutterstock_1488283535-6facec649168f734.jpg' data-full='https://heise.cloudimg.io/bound/1200x1200/q85.png-lossy-85.webp-lossy-85.foil1/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/1/1/6/1/8/2/shutterstock_1488283535-6facec649168f734.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neomindlabs.com/2026/06/08/running-gemma-4-26b-at-5-tokens-sec-on-a-13-year-old-xeon-with-no-gpu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running Gemma 4 26B at 5 tokens/sec on a 13-year-old Xeon with no GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在无 GPU 的13 年老旧 Xeon 上以每秒 5 token 运行 Gemma-4-26B</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma-4-26B runs at around 5 tokens per second on a 13-year-old Xeon with no GPU. This demonstrates impressive CPU-only efficiency for a 26B model, suggesting more affordable AI experimentation on commodity hardware. It also highlights how inference efficiency is catching up to model size.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma-4-26B 在没有 GPU 的13年老旧 Xeon 上以约每秒 5 token 的速度运行。这表明较大模型在纯 CPU 环境下的推理效率正在显著提升，为日常硬件上的 AI 实验提供了更低成本的可能性，同时也凸显了模型规模与推理效率的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.neomindlabs.com/images/we-taught-a-dead-server/gemma4-running.png' data-full='https://www.neomindlabs.com/images/we-taught-a-dead-server/gemma4-running.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把心理健康放在首位，以及沟通为何重要</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that mental health should be prioritized in tech work and that clear, frequent communication reduces burnout and misaligned expectations. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在科技行业要优先考虑心理健康，明确而频繁的沟通能降低倦怠感与目标错位。给出团队如何将心理健康对话常态化、个人如何设定界限的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LARP 为创业公司提供以收入为核心的基础设施，标准化定价、计费、分析和增长规划，帮助创始人实现可预测的收入增长。它被定位为相较随意变现更务实的方案，面向认真构建者。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenextweb.com/news/ai-advice-suppresses-critical-thinking-wrong-answers-study' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI advice made people less accurate but more confident – sudy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 助言让人更自信却更不准？研究指出</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds AI-provided advice boosts confidence while reducing accuracy, highlighting risks of overreliance and the need for careful design of decision-support interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，AI 提供的建议会提升自信但降低准确性，揭示过度依赖风险并强调在决策支持界面设计上的慎思。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48964015' data-tags='Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/stupside/castor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better and Cheaper Than IPTV</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比 IPTV 更好更便宜的流媒体方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Castor proposes a decentralized, open-source approach to streaming that aims to outperform traditional IPTV on cost and resilience. The project showcases how peer-to-peer tech and lightweight clients could reshape how video is delivered at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Castor 以去中心化、开源的流媒体方案为目标，力图在成本和鲁棒性上超过传统 IPTV。该项目展示了点对点技术与轻量客户端如何在大规模视频传输中重新定义交付方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1b82dd165cfce9c042ae655348a34f4fe3229417fd118efa4b0d656baf0924eb/stupside/castor' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1b82dd165cfce9c042ae655348a34f4fe3229417fd118efa4b0d656baf0924eb/stupside/castor' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48912175' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.alexedwards.net/blog/how-i-use-htmx-with-go' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I use HTMX with Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何在 Go 项目中使用 HTMX</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48912175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48912175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HTMX enables progressive enhancement by letting Go handlers drive partial page updates via HTML attributes, reducing front-end complexity while keeping fast, server-rendered UI. The post shares practical patterns for integrating HTMX with Go templates and endpoints to improve responsiveness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HTMX 通过在 HTML 属性中声明交互，让 Go 处理程序驱动局部页面更新，减少前端代码并保持服务器端渲染的快速 UI。文中分享将 HTMX 与 Go 模板和端点结合的实用模式，从而提升页面响应速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.alexedwards.net/static/img/cover-sm-lg.png' data-full='https://www.alexedwards.net/static/img/cover-sm-lg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48942000' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pydantic.dev/articles/the-human-in-the-loop-is-tired' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The human-in-the-loop is tired</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人机协同中的在环机制已疲惫</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48942000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48942000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that human-in-the-loop systems are reaching a fatigue point due to repetitive tasks and heavy cognitive load. It calls for redesigned tooling, clearer task boundaries, and smarter automation to keep humans in the loop where they add the most value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出在环人机系统正因重复性任务和认知负荷过大而感到疲惫。呼吁改进工具、划分清晰任务边界，并通过更智能的自动化来让人类在最具价值的环节保持参与。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pydantic.dev/articles/the-human-in-the-loop-is-tired/og.png' data-full='https://pydantic.dev/articles/the-human-in-the-loop-is-tired/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/supply/co-lab/work-louder/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex Micro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex Micro</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48923079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48923079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Codex Micro appears to be a compact coding-AI initiative from OpenAI, aiming to bring lightweight code-generation capabilities to smaller environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex Micro 看似为 OpenAI 的紧凑式编码AI，致力于将轻量级代码生成能力带入更小的运行环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3TMq0o4cH73IqJZA26rphi/d23e8a7f0fe28e447988bd59c79f0508/Frame_2147262134.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3TMq0o4cH73IqJZA26rphi/d23e8a7f0fe28e447988bd59c79f0508/Frame_2147262134.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/business/story/2026-07-16/spacex-stock-erases-gains-slides-below-ipo-price-in-intraday-trading' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX stock erases all its gains and slides below IPO price in intraday trading</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 股票盘中回吐涨幅并跌破发行价</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX stock erased earlier gains and traded below its IPO price intraday, reflecting renewed volatility or skepticism about the company’s fundraising prospects. The move highlights how a high-profile tech issuer can still swing with market sentiment, even after strong hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 股票盘中重新回吐全部涨幅，价格跌破发行价，显示市场情绪的再度波动。尽管此前备受瞩目，投资者对这家高知名度科技公司的融资前景仍持谨慎态度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/6a04202/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4367x2293+0+310/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fe6%2F5e%2Ff0d9b82c41a7b2c7d21d1d40a35c%2F175bee518a5a4f52b3535fdc3938398b.jpg' data-full='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/6a04202/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4367x2293+0+310/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fe6%2F5e%2Ff0d9b82c41a7b2c7d21d1d40a35c%2F175bee518a5a4f52b3535fdc3938398b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joinedanthropic.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>At least 105 past YC founders have worked at OpenAI and Anthropic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>至少有105名YC创始人曾在OpenAI和Anthropic任职</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48931588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48931588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>More than 105 YC founders have previously worked at OpenAI or Anthropic, illustrating how experience at leading AI labs feeds the next wave of startups. This talent mobility accelerates knowledge transfer in safety, scaling, and policy, while shaping the AI startup ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>超过105位 YC 创始人曾在 OpenAI 或 Anthropic 任职，显示出领先 AI 实验室的经验如何推动新一轮创业。人才流动加速了安全、扩展与政策领域的知识传递，并在 AI 初创生态中持续发力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://joinedanthropic.com/_next/image?url=%2Fwhere-now%2Favatars%2Fsam-altman.jpg&amp;w=96&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_Gomhz1FCH8xWKJ5vGZFqmU1tBGmY' data-full='https://joinedanthropic.com/_next/image?url=%2Fwhere-now%2Favatars%2Fsam-altman.jpg&amp;w=96&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_Gomhz1FCH8xWKJ5vGZFqmU1tBGmY' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48958269' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://environment.ec.europa.eu/news/ban-destruction-unsold-clothes-and-shoes-enters-application-2026-07-17_en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU ban on destruction of unsold clothes and shoes enters into application</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟禁止销毁未售出衣物和鞋履的规定正式生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48958269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48958269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s ban prohibits destroying unsold clothing and shoes, obliging brands to reuse, donate, or recycle excess stock. With this rule now in force, retailers must adjust inventory practices and supply chains to avoid waste. The move accelerates Europe’s push toward circular fashion and material recovery, with implications for margins and ESG reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟新规禁止销毁未售出衣物和鞋履，要求品牌将剩余库存用于再利用、捐赠或回收。此规定现已生效，促使零售商调整库存管理和供应链以减少浪费。此举推动时尚行业向循环经济与材料回收转型，可能影响利润与 ESG 报告。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://environment.ec.europa.eu/sites/default/files/styles/ewcms_metatag_image/public/2026-02/GettyImages-528424832.jpg?h=0d054e74&amp;itok=uUu2TLOk' data-full='https://environment.ec.europa.eu/sites/default/files/styles/ewcms_metatag_image/public/2026-02/GettyImages-528424832.jpg?h=0d054e74&amp;itok=uUu2TLOk' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shellzine.net/cyberpunk-comics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cyberpunk Comics, Manga and Graphic Novels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>赛博朋克题材的漫画、图像小说与漫画书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48885643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48885643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A survey of cyberpunk storytelling across comics, manga, and graphic novels, highlighting how artists translate neon-soaked futures into visual form. It shows how tech anxieties and subcultures shape contemporary visual narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梳理赛博朋克题材在漫画、图像小说中的呈现，聚焦艺术家如何将霓虹未来视觉语言转化为叙事。作品反映出科技焦虑与子文化对当代视觉叙事的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48827126' data-tags='Education,Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.susanrigetti.com/physics' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So you want to learn physics (second edition, 2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你想学习物理吗（第二版，2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical, self-guided path to physics with curated resources, emphasizing intuition and problem-solving. The second edition updates accessible topics and modern references, useful for developers and researchers who want solid physical grounding for AI, computation, or understanding the limits of technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个面向自学者的物理学习路线，强调直觉、问题解决和计算方法，并更新至 2021 年的第二版。内容精选、可操作，适合希望把物理思维融入 AI 和计算工作的人士参考。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48879504' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dakra.github.io/ghostel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghostel.el: Terminal emulator powered by libghostty</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghostel.el：由 libghostty 驱动的终端模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostel.el demonstrates a terminal emulator built inside Emacs powered by libghostty, aiming for fast, responsive performance and solid VT support. It shows how a native library can supercharge a Lisp environment without leaving the editor. For power users, it represents a compelling option for integrated terminal workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostel.el 展示了在 Emacs 内部、利用 libghostty 驱动的终端模拟器，目标是实现快速、高响应和完善的 VT 支持。它展示了原生库如何在不离开编辑器的前提下提升 Lisp 环境的性能。对于重度用户，这是一个集成终端工作流的有力选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://melpa.org/packages/ghostel-badge.svg' data-full='https://melpa.org/packages/ghostel-badge.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48927095' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.siegelendowment.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/fortune-david-siegel-open-source-ai.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Governments, companies, nonprofits should invest in free, open source AI [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府、企业、非营利组织应投资免费、开源的 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48927095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48927095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A policy brief argues that free, open-source AI is essential for public innovation, transparency, and resilience against vendor lock-in by governments, companies, and nonprofits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报告主张政府、企业与非营利组织应投资免费、开源的 AI，以促进创新、透明度并提升对单一供应商的抵御能力。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minecraft.net/en-us/article/minecraft-26-3-snapshot-4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minecraft: Java Edition now uses SDL3</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48967256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48967256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minecraft Java Edition has migrated its core to SDL3, replacing the previous multimedia layer. The shift modernizes the cross-platform stack and may reduce platform-specific bugs while enabling newer graphics and input features. This upgrade could pave the way for future performance improvements across Windows, macOS, and Linux.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Minecraft Java 版将核心迁移至 SDL3，替代旧的多媒体层。这一变更让跨平台栈更现代化，可能减少各平台的兼容性错误，并为引入新图形与输入特性创造条件。这或为 Windows、macOS 与 Linux 的后续性能优化铺路。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48951461' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://goliath32.com/blog/z80.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Zilog Z80 has turned 50</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zilog Z80 已迎来50周年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48951461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48951461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fifty years after its debut, the Z80 remains a touchstone in retro computing and embedded design. Its 8-bit architecture powered countless machines—from home computers to early game consoles—and its era-defining instruction set continues to influence hobbyist projects today. The milestone highlights the longevity and enduring influence of a well-crafted CPU design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自首次亮相50年前，Z80 已成为复古计算和嵌入式设计的重要基石。它的8位架构支撑了大量家用电脑和早期游戏主机，如今仍在爱好者项目中产生影响。这个纪念日凸显了一个精心设计的CPU在软硬件生态中的长期生命力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://goliath32.com/img/return.gif' data-full='https://goliath32.com/img/return.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lenergy.com.au/free-daytime-electricity-is-coming-heres-how-it-actually-works/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Australian energy retailers must offer three hours of free daytime electricity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>澳大利亚能源零售商须提供三小时免费 daytime 用电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48902320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48902320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 381</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Australian energy retailers are mandated to offer three hours of free daytime electricity, a policy designed to shift consumption to off-peak periods and improve grid stability. The article explains how it will work, who qualifies, and funding details.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该政策要求能源零售商在白天提供三小时免费用电，旨在将用电转向非高峰时段、提升电网稳定性。文中解释了执行方式、覆盖范围及资金来源。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48881874' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/leaders/2026/07/09/a-no-brainer-for-protecting-your-brain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The shingles vaccine may reduce the risk of dementia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带状疱疹疫苗或降低痴呆风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emerging evidence links shingles vaccination to a lower risk of developing dementia later in life, though researchers caution that correlation does not prove causation. If replicated, this could influence public health messaging and encourage older adults to complete the vaccine schedule. Researchers urge careful interpretation due to potential confounders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>初步证据显示带状疱疹疫苗与晚期痴呆风险降低相关，但相关性不等于因果关系。若结果被重复验证，可能影响公共卫生信息并鼓励老年人按时接种疫苗。研究者提醒需警惕混杂因素的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bikeshedding eats up team time by focusing on trivial design questions. The piece argues for sharper decision-making rituals, documenting decisions, and setting deadlines to prevent pointless debates from derailing work. It offers practical tips to keep discussions focused on high-impact outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文揭示团队常因琐碎设计问题而耗费大量时间，即所谓的自行车棚效应。作者主张通过更明确的决策流程、记录决策并设定时限来避免无谓辩论拖累进展。文章给出将讨论聚焦在高影响议题的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://improvesomething.today/responses-to-problems/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three ways people respond to a problem (other than solving it)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>除了真正解决问题之外，人们对问题的三种应对方式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three common non-solution responses to problems—avoidance, blame, and workaround-focused procrastination—which can derail teams. The piece offers perspectives on recognizing these patterns and steering efforts toward proactive problem-solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三种对待问题但并非解决本身的常见行为：回避、指责与应急式拖延，可能拖累团队进度。文中点出识别这些模式的重要性，并提出将注意力引导回到积极解决问题的路径。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ente.com/open/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ente – Opening Our Books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ente：公开我们的账本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48932697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48932697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ente is opening its books to transparency, inviting public scrutiny of its finances and governance. The move could build trust and reveal how resources are allocated.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dw.com/en/germany-freedom-of-information-act/a-77939695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Germany set to restrict its Freedom of Information Act</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国拟限制信息自由法的执行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany plans to tighten its Freedom of Information Act, narrowing the scope of data requests and adjusting processing rules. The move could curb public access to government documents, raising concerns about transparency and accountability. Journalists, researchers, and watchdogs may face added friction in seeking information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国计划收紧信息自由法（FOIA）的执行，缩小信息请求范围并调整处理规则。此举可能限制公众获取政府文件，引发对政府透明度与问责制的担忧。记者、研究者和监督机构在获取信息时可能遇到更高的门槛。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU court ruling curtails OpenAI’s ability to register certain branding or usage in Europe, potentially forcing changes to marketing or product naming. <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48943142' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/how-has-roman-concrete-lasted-for-millennia-a-1900-year-old-latrine-offers-new-clues-about-the-materials-impressive-durability-180989115/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Has Roman Concrete Lasted for Millennia? 1,900-Year-Old Latrine Offers Clues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>罗马混凝土为何能持久千年？1900 年历史的厕所揭示线索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48943142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48943142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of a 1,900-year-old Roman latrine reveals enduring durability due to a pozzolanic mix with volcanic ash that forms durable minerals in seawater. The findings offer lessons for modern, lower-carbon concretes and more durable infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一座1900年历史的罗马厕所进行分析，揭示其混凝土耐久性源于掺入火山灰等火山灰质材料的火山灰质混合物，在海水环境中会形成耐久矿物。研究为现代低碳混凝土设计和长期基础设施耐久性提供启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/ZudUQfnkrl8Fi4cI7JTX8GvHVxo=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1024x683:1025x684)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f8/ef/f8ef2bbf-1984-46d7-9fba-cb9324c82871/3917.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/ZudUQfnkrl8Fi4cI7JTX8GvHVxo=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1024x683:1025x684)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f8/ef/f8ef2bbf-1984-46d7-9fba-cb9324c82871/3917.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ploy.ai/blog/migrating-a-production-ai-agent-to-gpt-5-6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating a production AI agent to GPT-5.6: 2.2x faster, 27% cheaper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将生产级 AI 代理迁移至 GPT-5.6：速度提升 2.2 倍、成本降低 27%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A production-grade case study migrating an AI agent to GPT-5.6, delivering about 2.2x throughput and 27% cost savings. The write-up covers integration tweaks, latency considerations, and risk controls when upgrading models in production, offering practical guidance for teams planning a refresh.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇案例研究记录了把生产环境中的 AI 代理迁移到 GPT-5.6 的过程，吞吐量提升约 2.2 倍、成本下降约 27%。文末给出在生产环境升级模型时的集成优化、延迟考量和风险控制要点，为计划升级的团队提供实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48926939' data-tags='Show HN,Web,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.puter.com/labs/firefox-wasm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Firefox in WebAssembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：WebAssembly 实现的 Firefox</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48926939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48926939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox in WebAssembly demonstrates a full browser running inside WebAssembly. It highlights WASM’s potential for portable, sandboxed execution of large apps—and the challenges of performance, feature parity, and memory usage. For developers, it signals where WASM could enable new deployment models, edge runtimes, or sandboxed alternatives to native binaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WebAssembly 实现的 Firefox 展示了一个完整浏览器在 WebAssembly 环境中的运行。它凸显了将在大规模应用端口化到可移植运行时的潜力，但也暴露了性能、功能对等与内存使用方面的挑战。对开发者而言，这宽广了 WASM 的潜在用例，如新的部署模型、边缘运行时或对原生二进制的沙箱替代方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fabiensanglard.net/silpheed/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The art and engineering of Sega CD Silpheed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SEGA CD 的 Silpheed：艺术与工程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on Silpheed for the Sega CD, examining how the game combined art direction with early 3D graphics under hardware constraints. It highlights techniques like polygon models, texture work, and sound design that pushed the platform&#x27;s limits and influenced CG-driven console games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Sega CD 版 Silpheed，分析美术取向与早期 3D 图形在硬件限制下的结合。文章指出其在多边形模型、纹理与音效设计方面的运用，推动了当时对 CG 驱动的主机游戏的探索与影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48943745' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://journals.plos.org/plosbiology/article?id=10.1371/journal.pbio.3003876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EEG shows brain can simultaneous encode two speech streams</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>脑电图显示大脑能同时编码两路语音</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48943745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48943745' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers used EEG to demonstrate that the brain can track two concurrent speech streams, indicating parallel encoding rather than strictly serial processing. This challenges simple models of auditory processing and could influence future hearing-aid design and multi-speech decoding systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究通过脑电图证明大脑能够同时跟踪两路语音信号，表明存在并行编码而非串行处理。这一发现挑战了简单的听觉处理模型，并可能推动未来助听器设计和多语音解码系统的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://journals.plos.org/plosbiology/article/figure/image?id=10.1371/journal.pbio.3003876.g004&amp;size=inline' data-full='https://journals.plos.org/plosbiology/article/figure/image?id=10.1371/journal.pbio.3003876.g004&amp;size=inline' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 246</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moonshot AI pauses taking new subscriptions to meet demand for its Kimi K3, hinting at strong market interest in their AI accelerator or product. The move underscores supply constraints in specialized AI hardware/software ecosystems and the challenge of scaling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moonshot AI 因 Kimi K3 的高需求暂停接收新订阅，显示市场对其 AI 加速器/产品的强烈兴趣。此举也凸显在专业 AI 硬件与软件生态中扩产与供给的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post contends that kids with smartphones fare fine, offering data-driven perspectives on screen time, education, and social development. It argues responsible design and parental guidance matter more than device presence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为拥有手机的孩子们并非问题，结合数据观点探讨屏幕时间、教育与社交发展的关系。强调负责任的设计与家长引导比单纯禁用设备更为关键。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48956879' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://charlesazam.com/blog/fable-5-gpt-5-6-sol-goal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fable 5 vs. GPT-5.6 Sol on an NP-Hard Problem: Does /goal help?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fable 5 与 GPT-5.6 的 NP-hard 问题求解：/goal 是否有帮助？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A head-to-head comparison explores whether an explicit /goal directive helps when solving an NP-hard problem with Fable 5 vs GPT-5.6 Sol. It suggests goal-oriented prompting can improve search efficiency but may not guarantee an optimal solution, highlighting the nuance of AI-assisted problem solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章比较了 Fable 5 与 GPT-5.6 Sol 在一个 NP-hard 问题上的求解表现，重点是 /goal 指令是否提升效率。结果表明，明确的目标导向提示可能改变搜索策略，影响解的质量与速度，这对 AI 辅助问题求解具有实际启示。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/on-prem/2026/07/11/irish-datacenters-now-guzzle-23-of-the-countrys-electricity/5270013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Irish datacenters now guzzle 23% of the country&#x27;s electricity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>爱尔兰数据中心现已消耗全国约 23% 的电力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48884322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 303</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ireland&#x27;s data centers now account for about 23% of the country&#x27;s electricity demand, a share that has grown with hyperscale deployments. The heavy load on the grid raises concerns about energy efficiency, capacity planning, and emissions, prompting policymakers to rethink incentives for renewables and cooling optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱尔兰的数据中心现约占全国电力需求的23%，随着海量数据中心的扩张，这一比例持续上升。对电网的高强度负荷引发关于能效、容量规划和排放的担忧，促进决策者重新审视可再生能源、冷却效率等激励措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/4093897.jpg?imageId=4093897&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=95.56&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=95.56&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/4093897.jpg?imageId=4093897&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=95.56&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=95.56&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openculture.com/2026/07/explore-1300-beautiful-wildlife-illustrations-from-the-19th-century.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1,300 Beautiful Wildlife Illustrations from the 19th Century Now Restored</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vast restoration project brings 1,300 19th-century wildlife illustrations back to life, digitized for study and enjoyment. The collection highlights historical natural history art and the value of restoration work for access and education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>修复工作让19世纪1300幅野生动物插图重现光彩，数字化版本便于研究与公众欣赏。展示修复在保护美术与自然史资料、提升可访问性方面的价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48936880' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.lyc8503.net/en/post/llm-classifier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Detecting LLM-Generated Texts with “Classical” Machine Learning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用传统机器学习检测大语言模型生成文本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Even with powerful LLMs, traditional machine learning classifiers can help detect AI-generated text using features like syntax patterns and punctuation. The approach often provides interpretable signals that complement neural detectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>即使面对强大的大语言模型，传统机器学习分类器也能通过句法模式、标点等特征识别 AI 生成文本，提供可解释的信号，常与神经检测相互补充。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.lyc8503.net/en/post/llm-classifier/ppl.png' data-full='https://blog.lyc8503.net/en/post/llm-classifier/ppl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://madcampos.dev/blog/2026/07/accessibility-from-scratch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you want to create a button from scratch, you must first create the universe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你想从零开始设计一个按钮，首先要创造宇宙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 239</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that true accessibility requires rethinking the button from its foundations—semantics, roles, and keyboard focus—before styling. It presents a mental model for designing controls that are usable by everyone, from screen readers to assistive tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张要从根本上重新设计无障碍按钮，先明确语义、角色和键盘聚焦，再考虑样式。给出一套面向屏幕阅读器等辅助技术的设计思路，帮助开发者从一开始就实现真正可用的控件。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gleam, a lightweight language/tool for BEAM, is now available on Tangled, broadening where developers can experiment with it. The move expands the Gleam ecosystem by enabling another platform to host and run Gleam projects. This kind of platform expansion helps smaller runtimes reach a bigger developer base.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gleam 这门适用于 BEAM 的轻量级语言/工具如今已在 Tangled 平台上线，扩展了开发者的实验场景。此举将 Gleam 的生态带到新的平台，方便在不同环境中运行 Gleam 项目。平台层面的扩展有助于小型运行时触及更多开发者。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48952565' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/BadChemical/IoT-Vulnerability-Research-Public/blob/main/TP-Link_Kasa_EC71/Kasa_EC71.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TP-Link Kasa cameras leaked home GPS via unauthenticated UDP for 6 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TP-Link Kasa 摄像头六年来通过未认证的 UDP 泄露家庭位置信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 cameras were exposed to a long‑standing vulnerability where unauthenticated UDP traffic could leak sensitive GPS data. The issue underscores risks in legacy IoT designs and the need for firmware updates and network isolation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 摄像头长期存在未认证的 UDP 漏洞，可能泄露家庭位置信息。此事凸显了旧式物联网设计的风险，以及对固件更新和网络隔离的迫切需要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b109ed0a1ab99434f27464484d7cbd39236dfe5e7e536c7abac1559e66d49512/BadChemical/IoT-Vulnerability-Research-Public' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b109ed0a1ab99434f27464484d7cbd39236dfe5e7e536c7abac1559e66d49512/BadChemical/IoT-Vulnerability-Research-Public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security bulletin warns that insecure argument handling in Tailscale SSH could allow an attacker to gain root access. The advisory urges upgrading to patched versions and applying mitigations until fixes propagate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全公告警示 Tailscale SSH 的参数处理不安全，可能使恶意者获得 root 权限。建议尽早升级至修补版本，执行缓解措施，直至全面修复。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/w77i7m8x/production/8e0455b2d9b33c6151016afdf2ea81d7623c2f04-1200x628.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/w77i7m8x/production/8e0455b2d9b33c6151016afdf2ea81d7623c2f04-1200x628.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现有最大的 Minecraft 世界：总容量达 15 TB</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48872401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48872401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The largest available Minecraft world spans 15 TB, pushing storage, streaming, and mod ecosystems to their limits. Such massive worlds stress hosting infrastructure, data integrity, and distribution challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>总容量达 15 TB 的最大 Minecraft 世界展示了玩家社区在像素世界规模化方面的极限，考验存储、流式传输和模组生态系统的能力，也带来托管、数据一致性与分发的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The arXiv preprint surveys core mathematical themes in data science, including optimization, statistics, high-dimensional geometry, and generalization. It connects theory to practical algorithm design and highlights where intuition can mislead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 arXiv 预印本梳理数据科学中的核心数学主题，如优化、统计、高维几何与泛化等，并将理论与实际算法设计联系起来，指出直觉可能误导的地方。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
