<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://goughlui.com/2026/07/09/teardown-a-generic-7-port-usb-3-0-hub-that-wasnt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teardown: A Generic 7-Port USB 3.0 Hub That Wasn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拆解：一个看起来通用的7端口USB 3.0集线器原来并非如此</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on teardown reveals a 7-port USB 3.0 hub that doesn’t live up to its generic claim, exposing a nonstandard controller and quirks in power management. The teardown underscores how budget hubs can mislead and what to look for when buying cheap USB hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拆解揭示这款7端口的USB 3.0 集线器虽然标称通用，内部却使用非标控制芯片并在供电管理上存在问题。对比便宜硬件的常见陷阱，提醒读者在选购低价外设时要留意内在实现与稳定性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://goughlui.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/202607022204196627.jpg' data-full='https://goughlui.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/202607022204196627.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/mathematicians-still-dont-know-the-fastest-way-to-multiply-numbers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mathematicians still don&#x27;t know the fastest way to multiply numbers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学家仍未搞清最快的乘法方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48895861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48895861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite decades of progress, the definitive fastest algorithm for multiplying large numbers remains unknown. Practical approaches combine sophisticated algorithms with implementation tricks, leaving room for both theoretical and hardware-driven improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有长期进展，求解大数乘法的绝对最快算法仍然未知。实践上采用复杂的算法结合实现技巧，理论与硬件层面的改进仍有空间。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/1caac706-e03e-4760-8ef0-cc5a554737df/GettyImages-168351286-math-chalkboard.jpeg?m=1783946841.043&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/1caac706-e03e-4760-8ef0-cc5a554737df/GettyImages-168351286-math-chalkboard.jpeg?m=1783946841.043&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48903082' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cg53l737v1qo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Indian scientists produce most detailed 3D atlas of the human brainstem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>印度科学家绘制出最详细的人脑脑干三维解剖图谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Indian researchers have produced the most detailed 3D atlas of the human brainstem to date, enabling finer mapping of its nuclei and connectivity. The atlas promises to aid neurosurgery planning, neuroimaging, and neuroscience research by providing higher-resolution references. This milestone showcases advances in imaging and international collaboration in brain mapping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>印度研究团队创造了迄今最详细的人脑脑干三维解剖图谱，提升了对脑干核团及连接关系的细致刻画能力。该图谱有望帮助神经外科手术规划、神经影像学研究，以及神经科学研究，提供更高分辨率的参考资料。此成就体现了成像技术和全球协作在脑图绘制领域的进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/8230/live/dff3e310-79da-11f1-a400-b302bc872d47.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/8230/live/dff3e310-79da-11f1-a400-b302bc872d47.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>强化学习小册子</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48913050' data-tags='DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-07-14-dependabot-version-updates-introduce-default-package-cooldown/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dependabot version updates introduce default package cooldown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dependabot 版本更新引入默认软件包冷却期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dependabot now enforces a default cooldown between updates of the same package to reduce update churn and stabilize downstream projects. Teams can adjust or disable this cooldown if needed, but the default helps limit noisy PRs and rate-limit pressure. This change aims to balance security fixes with operational stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dependabot 对同一软件包的更新引入了默认冷却期，目的是减少更新带来的噪声并稳定下游项目。团队若有需要可以调整或禁用该冷却策略，但默认设置有助于限制冗杂的拉取请求及速率压力。这一变动旨在在安全修复与运营稳定之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/themes/github-2021-child/dist/img/social-v3-improvements.jpg' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/themes/github-2021-child/dist/img/social-v3-improvements.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Guerrilla ad campaign in London mocks Kylie Jenner’s Meta glasses push, blending street art with tech skepticism. The stunt spotlights growing tension between flashy AR hardware launches and consumer privacy concerns.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：有哪些已知的 AWS 计费问题？亚马逊对 AWS 的支出预测为30亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>

The takeaway is to implement proactive cost controls and continuous monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论聚焦于 AWS 计费问题及潜在的未预期费用，揭示常见的云成本陷阱（如未打标签的资源、数据传输费等）并给出预算、警报和治理的实用建议。核心在于建立主动的成本控制与持续监控。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Precursor</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/microsoft-patches-a-record-570-security-flaws/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft has released software updates to plug at least 570 security holes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软发布补丁，修复至少570个安全漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft released a record patch batch addressing 570+ vulnerabilities across Windows, Office, and related products. Many fixes resolve remote code execution and privilege escalation flaws; users should apply updates promptly to reduce exposure to attackers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软发布大量安全更新，修复超过570处漏洞，涵盖 Windows、Office 等产品。多数修复针对远程代码执行与提权等风险，建议尽快打补丁以降低被攻击的概率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prathosh.in/vagdhenu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vāgdhenu: A Sanskrit Chanting TTS System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vāgdhenu：梵文诵唱文本转语音系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vāgdhenu is an open-source Sanskrit chanting text-to-speech system, enabling natural recitations for language preservation and education. It highlights niche TTS work that supports less-resourced languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vāgdhenu 是一个开源梵文诵唱文本转语音系统，便于自然朗诵用于语言保护和教育。该项目凸显了对资源匮乏语言的 TTS 支持。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://softwaredoug.com/blog/2026/07/09/write-code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why write code in 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么在 2026 年写代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>By 2026, coding is shifting from typing boilerplate to composing with AI-assisted tools and high-level abstractions. The piece argues that success will hinge on architectural thinking, system design, and effective collaboration, not just syntax. It offers pragmatic tips for adapting to this evolving workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>到 2026 年，编码将从大量重复性敲击转向使用 AI 辅助工具和高层抽象进行组合。文章认为成功取决于架构思维、系统设计和高效协作，而不仅仅是语法。并给出适应这一演变工作流的实用建议。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.texasattorneygeneral.gov/news/releases/attorney-general-ken-paxton-secures-landmark-legal-victory-lock-pornographic-website-domain-and' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Texas wins court order to suspend domain name for violating age-verification law</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德州法院裁定暂停因年龄验证法违规的域名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Texas won a court order to suspend a domain for violating age-verification requirements for adult content, signaling aggressive enforcement of online identity policies. The ruling highlights the tension between access and compliance online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德州法院裁定暂停因违反年龄验证法而相关的域名，显示对在线身份政策的强力执法。此裁决凸显了网络访问与合规之间的紧张关系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.texasattorneygeneral.gov/sites/default/files/images/admin/images/2019/PR-Twitter-Card.png' data-full='https://www.texasattorneygeneral.gov/sites/default/files/images/admin/images/2019/PR-Twitter-Card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://core.telegram.org/bots/serverless' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Telegram Serverless</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Telegram launches serverless bot infrastructure enabling developers to deploy bots without managing servers, lowering barriers to building scalable bots on Telegram.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Telegram 发布无服务端的 Bot 基础设施，开发者无需自管服务器即可部署可扩展的机器人应用，降低开发门槛。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scotto.me/blog/2026-07-17-which-lisp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Road to Lisp: Which Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>走向 Lisp 的路：到底选哪一种 Lisp？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Road to Lisp surveys the Lisp family and tooling to help readers decide which dialect fits their goals. It weighs factors like macro systems, ecosystems, syntax, and interoperability. The piece clarifies Lisp choices beyond the familiar Common Lisp and Scheme paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理 Lisp 家族及工具链，帮助读者根据目标选择合适的方言。比较宏系统、生态、语法与互操作性等要点，超越对 Common Lisp 或 Scheme 的常规认知，给出选型指引。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/17/faa-boeing-737-max-787.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FAA lets Boeing sign off on 737 MAX, 787 airworthiness certificates again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FAA 再次允许波音就 737 MAX 与 787 发放适航证书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Regulatory oversight has allowed Boeing to sign off on airworthiness certificates for the 737 MAX and 787 again, signaling a normalization after extensive safety reviews. The move underscores how certification processes can rebound after high-profile scrutiny.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>监管机构允许波音再次就 737 MAX 和 787 发放适航证书，标志着在广泛安全审查后的规范化过程重新开始。此举凸显了在高关注度调查后，认证流程有望回归常态。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/07/14/data-centers-23-billion-electricity-bills/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Data centers have hiked electricity prices on the public by $23B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数据中心抬高电价，致公众多付约230亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48914683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48914683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Fortune report puts a number on data center energy costs, finding that electricity pricing tied to data center demand has shifted about $23 billion of costs onto the public. The rise reflects grid strain, peak demand, and policy dynamics around energy and data infrastructure. As workloads grow, efficiency, locality decisions, and regulatory oversight become critical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份 Fortune 报告给出数据中心能源成本的数字，称与数据中心需求相关的电价上升使公众承担约230亿美元的额外电费。上涨源于电网紧张、峰值需求和能源与数据基础设施的政策环境。随着数据负载增加，能效、选址与监管成为关键议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/GettyImages-2285284906-e1783971804803.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/GettyImages-2285284906-e1783971804803.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.zocalopublicsquare.org/my-midlife-crisis-corolla-fast-furious-fully-modded/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My midlife crisis Corolla is fast, furious, and modded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的中年危机卡罗拉：快、狂、改装</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48921303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48921303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 417</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay about a fast, heavily modified Toyota Corolla, reflecting the satisfaction and risks of car tinkering in midlife. It touches on identity, community, and the joys of hands-on engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于被改装的丰田卡罗拉的个人随笔，讨论中年阶段通过动手改装寻求自我认同的过程，以及相关的风险与乐趣。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48895199' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://solod.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solod: Go can be a better C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Solod：Go 可能比 C 更适合系统编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48895199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48895199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solod argues that Go, with safer memory management and productive tooling, can outperform C for many systems-programming tasks when used with careful unsafe code and zero-cost abstractions. The piece outlines a path for Go to supplant C in some domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Solod 认为在多种系统编程场景下，Go 具备更安全的内存管理与高效的工具链，若在需要时谨慎使用 unsafe 和零成本抽象，Go 也能超越 C 的生产力。文章勾勒了 Go 在部分领域替代 C 的可行路线。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48889350' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/07/another-ridiculous-interrail-holiday-6379km-and-13-countries-over-7-weeks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Interrail: 6,379Km and 13 Countries over 7 weeks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>跨欧洲铁路之旅：6379 公里、13 国、7 周</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long Interrail trip covers 6,379km across 13 countries in seven weeks, offering practical insights on travel planning, budgeting, and rail-based itineraries for tech travelers. The journey reveals how to balance pace, logistics, and curiosity when exploring Europe by train.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过跨欧洲火车通票完成 6379 公里、13 个国家的七周旅程，提供对旅行规划、预算与铁路线路的实用见解。 行程展示了在欧洲铁路旅行中如何兼顾节奏、后勤与探索欲。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Long-Interrail-Journey.webp' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Long-Interrail-Journey.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Last-MPEG-4-Patent-Expired' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Last MPEG-4 Visual Patent Has Expired</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>With the last MPEG-4 Visual patent expiration, licensing barriers ease for developers and hardware makers, potentially boosting open implementations and lower-cost codecs. The change could influence licensing models and drive broader adoption of MPEG-4 Visual technologies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着最后一个 MPEG-4 Visual 专利到期，开发者与硬件厂商在许可方面的障碍将减少，可能推动开源实现与成本更低的编解码方案。此变动或促使对 MPEG-4 Visual 技术的更广泛采用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 声音欺诈能在短短三秒内仿冒他人声音，绕过部分防御措施。文章呼吁升级认证与提升用户教育以应对这种高效的社交工程攻击。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://curiouscoding.nl/posts/static-search-tree/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Static search trees: 40x faster than binary search (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>静态搜索树：2024 年比二分查找快 40 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48951898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48951898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Static search trees claim up to 40x speedups over binary search for certain workloads by precomputing query structures. The approach could reshape performance-critical code paths in compilers, databases, and real-time systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>静态搜索树声称在某些工作负载下比二分查找快多达 40 倍，通过预计算查询结构实现。此方法可能改变编译器、数据库和实时系统中的关键性能路径。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry. Such claims are subjected to rigorous verification, as a verified counterexample would upend established theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。此类主张需要严格验证，一旦成立将彻底改变相关理论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://consequence.net/2026/07/the-cd-revival-is-getting-hard-to-ignore/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CD sales growth outpaced vinyl in the first half of 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年前六个月 CD 销售增速超过黑胶唱片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48937398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48937398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Compact discs posted faster growth than vinyl in the first half of 2026, signaling a small but real revival in physical media. Industry observers point to nostalgia, pricing dynamics, and collector activity as drivers, with implications for retailers and artists alike. The trend matters for how physical formats coexist with streaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年上半年，CD 销量增速超过黑胶，显示物理介质的复兴虽小但确有迹象。分析称怀旧情绪、定价与收藏热是推动力，这对零售商和艺术家均有影响，也揭示了实体格式与流媒体的共存趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lobste.rs/s/ko1ji1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lobste.rs is now running on SQLite</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48899847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48899847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community-driven site transfers its backend to SQLite, prioritizing simplicity and portability over a heavy database stack. While this can simplify maintenance, it also invites scrutiny on performance under heavy traffic and large discussion threads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个社区驱动的网站将后端迁移到 SQLite，强调简单性和便携性胜过繁重的数据库栈。虽有维护便利，但在高流量与大量讨论串的场景下，性能表现需要关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48906125' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jacobfilipp.com/care/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Proof of care in the age of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 时代的关怀证明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48906125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48906125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In the AI era, the author argues that care must be embedded in technology design and deployment, not treated as an afterthought. The piece advocates practical ways to bake care into systems—transparent safety practices, accountability, and user-centered design—so AI serves people beyond efficiency. It calls for thinking about long-term social impacts in engineering decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 AI 时代，本文主张将“关怀”嵌入技术设计与部署之中，而非事后补足。文章提出将关怀体现在系统中的具体做法，如透明的安全实践、明确的问责制，以及以用户为中心的设计，以让 AI 超越单纯的效率，服务于人类的长期利益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jacobfilipp.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image-33.png' data-full='https://jacobfilipp.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/image-33.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48901454' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://web.stanford.edu/~dntse/wireless_book.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fundamentals of Wireless Communication (2005)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无线通信基础</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise, foundational guide to wireless communication theory, covering signal propagation, modulation, coding, and multi-user channels. It helps engineers and students reason about spectrum use, interference, and system design tradeoffs across modern wireless networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本基础教材系统讲解了无线通信的理论与工程要点，包括信号传播、调制、编码以及多用户信道等，帮助读者理解频谱利用、干扰与系统设计取舍。对从事5G/6G、物联网等领域的工程师和学生尤为有益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://web.stanford.edu/~dntse/book2.jpg' data-full='https://web.stanford.edu/~dntse/book2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48966984' data-tags='Web,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.andros.dev/blog/0b8e451e/i-joined-the-indieweb-heres-what-i-learned/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I joined the IndieWeb, here&#x27;s what I learned</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Joining the IndieWeb, the author reflects on ownership, portability, and interoperability of web content, as well as practical steps to start hosting your own site. The piece offers insights for readers curious about a more self-sovereign online presence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加入 IndieWeb 后，作者反思了网页内容的所有权、可移植性与互操作性，以及自建站点的实际步骤。文中给出对追求更自主在线存在感的读者有用的见解。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/nokia-phones-history' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nokia’s years of mobile-phone supremacy ended in an afternoon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>诺基亚在一个下午就失去了长期的手机霸主地位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look back at how Nokia’s decades-long handset dominance collapsed in an afternoon as smartphones and ecosystems disrupted the market. The piece analyzes strategic missteps, a slow software pivot, and the rise of platform economies that left hardware leaders behind. It offers sobering lessons on software strategy and ecosystem alignment for hardware companies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾诺基亚在一个下午就失去长期手机霸主地位的过程：智能手机与生态系统的崛起彻底改变了市场格局。文章解析了战略失误、对软件转型的迟缓，以及以平台为核心的生态系统如何让硬件领导者落后。为硬件企业提供了对软件策略与生态系统协同的深刻教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/two-young-girls-stand-in-front-of-a-glass-case-containing-2000-era-cellphones-behind-them-is-a-display-that-says-nokia.jpg?id=67118775&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C911%2C0%2C912' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/two-young-girls-stand-in-front-of-a-glass-case-containing-2000-era-cellphones-behind-them-is-a-display-that-says-nokia.jpg?id=67118775&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C911%2C0%2C912' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://honeypotlive.cc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Watch bots interact with an SSH honeypot in real time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：看机器人与 SSH 蜜罐实时互动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Live footage of bots interacting with an SSH honeypot reveals attacker automation patterns and defender signals in real time. The stream provides tangible lessons for threat hunting, anomaly detection, and hardened SSH configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实时演示显示机器人与 SSH 蜜罐的互动，揭示攻击者的自动化行为与防守信号。此直播为威胁猎杀、异常检测和 SSH 安全加固提供了具体的教训。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS：家暴受害者的安全选择</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48942454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48942454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS is recommended for domestic-abuse victims due to its strong security, privacy controls, and app hardening that reduce risk when devices are under coercive control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 因具备强健的安全性、隐私控制和应用加固等特性，被推荐给家暴受害者，以降低在控制环境中被监控或数据暴露的风险。文章举例说明其对应用隔离与低数据回传等的实际保护作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.normaltech.ai/p/what-will-be-left-for-us-to-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What will be left for us to work on?</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As automation and AI advance, the piece questions what meaningful human work will remain and how to re-skill. It discusses shifts in labor markets, creativity, care work, and the role of policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着自动化和人工智能的发展，探讨未来人类可以从事的有意义工作，以及如何通过再培训应对劳动力市场的变动。关注创造性、关怀类工作和政策作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48934286' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.spaceweather.gov/news/goes-19-safe-hold' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Goes-19 weather satellite enters Safe Hold mode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GOES-19 天气卫星进入安全待机模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48934286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48934286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GOES-19 has entered Safe Hold mode after a detected anomaly, pausing normal ops. In Safe Hold, the satellite conserves power and awaits ground control to restore full operations, potentially reducing near-term weather data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GOES-19 天气卫星因检测到异常进入安全待机模式，暂停常规操作。处于安全待机状态时，卫星会节省能源并等待地面控制重新启动全性能作业，可能短暂影响近实时天气数据的获取。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.spaceweather.gov/themes/custom/swpc/images/logos/noaa_logo.svg' data-full='https://www.spaceweather.gov/themes/custom/swpc/images/logos/noaa_logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JetBrains/youtrackdb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>YouTrackDB is a general-use object-oriented graph database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YouTrackDB：通用型面向对象图数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48902026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48902026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YouTrackDB positions itself as a general-use object-oriented graph database, blending OO data modeling with graph traversal capabilities. It aims to offer a unified API for nodes and edges, appealing to developers who want both OOP ergonomics and rich graph queries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YouTrackDB 将自己定位为通用型的面向对象图数据库，将面向对象的数据建模与图遍历能力结合，提供一个统一的节点/边操作 API。对同时追求 OOP 体验与丰富图查询的开发者而言，这类数据库能简化数据建模与关系表达。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7074981b212f29daf7f67ce3f939fbfa0d0556e9d6be73e2762c70f28040dfdb/JetBrains/youtrackdb' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7074981b212f29daf7f67ce3f939fbfa0d0556e9d6be73e2762c70f28040dfdb/JetBrains/youtrackdb' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>协作的力量：如何减少交通拥堵</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Research outlines how collaboration among cities, companies, and researchers can reduce traffic congestion through shared data, coordinated policies, and joint experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌研究指出，城市、企业与研究机构的协作通过数据共享、协同政策和联合试验，能够降低交通拥堵。文章强调通过数据共享、仿真等实际路径推动全系统的改进。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open Book Touch is an open-source e-reader funded by crowdsourcing, aiming to provide a customizable, privacy-respecting alternative to proprietary devices. It emphasizes openness in hardware, software, and content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Open Book Touch 是一个通过众筹资助的开源电子书阅读器，旨在提供可定制、注重隐私的替代方案。强调硬件、软件与内容的开放性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c79gw4lj89eo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump Media to sell instant access to &#x27;market-moving&#x27; social posts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普媒体将出售“即时访问”能引发市场波动的社交帖子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48944487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48944487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trump Media plans to monetize instant access to market-moving social posts, selling real-time, potentially price-sensitive content. The move raises questions about market integrity, disclosure norms, and platform monetization in politically charged contexts. It signals how real-time content could become a financial product.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普媒体计划将“即时访问”可能影响市场的社交帖子变现，出售实时且可能涉及价格敏感的信息。此举引发市场公信力、披露规范及在政治场景中的平台变现等问题，反映了实时内容或许成为一种金融产品的趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/media-telecom/short-sellers-rack-up-87-bln-profit-spacex-slips-below-ipo-price-ortex-2026-07-16/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Short sellers notch $8.7B profit as SpaceX shares dip to IPO price</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>做空者在 SpaceX 股价跌回 IPO 价位时赚取 87 亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48948435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48948435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Short sellers posted about $8.7B in profits as SpaceX shares drifted back toward their IPO price. The move underscores how even high-growth tech names can see sharp pullbacks that reward bears, revealing investment dynamics around private-to-public transitions and valuation skepticism. It highlights market volatility and the limits of hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>做空者在 SpaceX 股价回落至 IPO 价位附近时实现约87亿美元利润，展现出高成长科技股也可能因估值回落而让做空方获利的市场特性，反映从私有到上市过程中的估值怀疑与投资者情绪波动。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48913443' data-tags='Finance,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bis.org/publ/bisbull120.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Financing the AI boom: from cash flows to debt [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为 AI 繁荣融资：从现金流到债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The BIS paper surveys financing the AI boom, arguing that funding may shift from cash flows and equity to debt instruments as AI demand grows. It highlights potential risks for financial stability and the need for prudent risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BIS 的论文探讨 AI 繁荣的融资结构，指出资金来源可能从现金流与股权转向债务工具，并评估对金融稳定的潜在风险及需加强的风险管理。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何选用 Vanilla JavaScript？</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for sticking with plain JavaScript over frameworks, emphasizing simplicity, portability, and long-term maintainability. It warns that over-engineered toolchains often add overhead without proportional payoff.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张坚持使用纯 JavaScript 而非框架，强调简洁性、可移植性和长期维护性，以及避免被过度复杂的工具链拖累。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://imdanielkendall.com/the-great-software-regress-how-move-fast-and-break-things-broke-our-lives/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My car’s OTA update broke Android Auto</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的车载 OTA 更新把 Android Auto 搞坏了</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-car OTA update broke Android Auto, disrupting users’ connected experience. The incident highlights the fragility of software ecosystems in modern vehicles and the need for robust regression testing, clear rollback paths, and careful update governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次车载系统 OTA 更新导致 Android Auto 功能失效，打乱了用户的智能互联体验。此事凸显现代汽车软件生态的脆弱性，强调需要更可靠的回归测试、可回滚机制以及谨慎的更新治理。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ikea.greg.technology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: IKEA Complexity Index</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IKEA 复杂度指数：家具组装难度的量化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48955042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48955042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN reveals a project that assigns a Complexity Index to IKEA products, quantifying how hard they are to assemble. By combining product data and assembly steps, the index helps shoppers compare difficulty and could influence design choices or assembly tooling. It also opens discussion about how much assembly complexity drives consumer perception and resale value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 推出一个项目，将 IKEA 产品的复杂度量化为一个复杂度指数，用以评估组装难度。通过整合产品数据与组装步骤，该指数帮助用户对比难度，或影响设计选择和组装工具开发。该项目还引发关于组装复杂度如何影响消费体验与二手交易价值的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48907296' data-tags='Politics,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/new-york-becomes-first-state-impose-data-center-moratorium-2026-07-14/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New York becomes the first state to impose a data center moratorium</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约成为首个对数据中心实施暂停令的州</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York becomes the first state to impose a moratorium on new data center development, signaling concerns about energy demand, grid capacity, and climate goals. The pause may slow cloud capacity expansion and influence investment strategies for regional operators. Regulators and industry players will watch how the policy balance growth with energy resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约成为首个对数据中心实施暂停令的州，体现出对能源需求、电网容量及气候目标的关注。暂停可能放缓本地云基础设施扩张，并影响区域运营商的投资策略。监管机构与业界将关注此类政策在促进增长与提升能源韧性之间的平衡。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece uses the computer found at the bottom of a canal to reflect on hardware longevity, data preservation, and the fragility of digital artifacts. It invites thinking about how long equipment lasts in harsh environments and what that means for archives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章以在运河底部发现的计算机为切入点，探讨硬件耐久性、数据保存与数字遗产的脆弱性。通过这个实例，思考设备在极端环境中的寿命及对档案保存的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Reynard 是一个在 iOS 13 及以上版本上提供真正 Firefox 风格浏览器的尝试，绕过了苹果对浏览器引擎的限制。这个项目体现了开源方法在苹果设备上推动替代浏览体验的潜力与挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide showing how to train a generative AI kick-drum model on a consumer-grade GPU (6GB VRAM) using compact architectures and optimization tricks. <p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/welcome-to-the-sunny-side/misa77' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: misa77 - a codec that decodes 2x faster than LZ4 (at better ratios)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：misa77——一个解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍、压缩比更佳的编解码器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>misa77 is a codec that lands faster decoding than LZ4 with better ratios. It challenges assumptions about speed vs. compression trade-offs and could influence storage and network efficiency in real-world apps. The release provides insights into practical codec design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>misa77 是一个编解码器，解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍且压缩效果更好。这对存储与网络传输效率提出了新的可能，也展示了编码器设计中的权衡与取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48937288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48937288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A striking 42% of adults rely on parental financial support, reflecting rising living costs and debt pressures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>约有 42% 的成年人仍依赖父母提供经济援助，反映日益上涨的生活成本和债务压力。文章探讨此现象对长期理财与独立性的影响，并结合金融治疗师的建议，提出建立健康边界与有序支持的思路。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前沿模型的真实价格</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It analyzes the true costs of frontier AI models, factoring in training compute, data licensing, inference costs, and operational overhead, showing that headline prices often underestimate total cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析前沿 AI 模型的真实成本，涵盖训练计算、数据许可、推理成本与运维等，揭示了标称价格背后的隐性开销，提醒企业在采购时考虑总拥有成本。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isene.org/2026/07/Frame.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Frame – Linux X server in Assembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Frame：在汇编中实现的 Linux X 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48948597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48948597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Frame is an experiment building an X server for Linux entirely in assembly language, pushing the boundaries of low-level optimization. The project delves into performance considerations and the trade-offs of hand-written assembly in a modern GUI stack. It serves as a provocative exploration rather than a practical replacement for current servers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Frame 是一个在 Linux 上用汇编语言实现 X 服务器的实验，旨在探索底层优化的极限。该项目讨论了性能考量以及手写汇编在现代 GUI 架构中的权衡，更多是一种挑衅性探索而非现有服务器的实际替代方案。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ca.pcmag.com/social-media/16790/the-death-of-the-status-update-why-55-of-americans-stopped-posting-on-social-media' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Death of the Status Update: Why 55% of Americans Stopped Posting on Social Media</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>状态更新的时代结束：为何55%的美国人停止在社交媒体发帖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48879902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demographics, platform changes, and privacy fatigue help explain why 55% of Americans stopped posting on social media. The trend has implications for marketers, content creators, and platform design as attention shifts and new formats emerge. It underscores a pivot point in social communication patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人口结构变化、平台演变与隐私疲劳共同解释了为何约55%的美国人停止在社交媒体发帖。这一趋势将影响营销、内容创作者与平台设计，因为受众重心正在转移，新格式也在兴起，揭示社交沟通模式的转折点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sm.pcmag.com/t/pcmag_ca/news/t/the-death-/the-death-of-the-status-update-why-55-of-americans-stopped-p_esbq.1200.png' data-full='https://sm.pcmag.com/t/pcmag_ca/news/t/the-death-/the-death-of-the-status-update-why-55-of-americans-stopped-p_esbq.1200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andiroberts.com/citizenship/the-social-physics-of-conversation-citizenship-leadership' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The social physics of conversation: Communication patterns matter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对话的社会物理学：沟通模式很重要</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48828576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48828576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Conversations follow measurable patterns that influence trust, collaboration, and leadership legitimacy. By analyzing social dynamics, the piece argues that tuning communication styles can produce tangible improvements in teamwork and civic engagement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对话遵循可衡量的模式，直接影响信任、协作与领导力的可信度。通过分析社交动态，文章主张调整沟通风格可以显著提升团队协作和公民参与的效果。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lego.com/en-us/history/articles/d-lego-building-instructions-through-time' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lego building instructions through time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>乐高搭建指令的历史演变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48950518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48950518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lego building instructions have evolved from paper manuals to digital formats, influencing how builders learn and construct. The evolution reflects broader shifts in accessibility, standards, and user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乐高搭建指令从纸质手册演变到数字化形式，影响了玩家的学习与搭建方式。此次演变反映了可访问性、标准化和用户体验的更广泛变革。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48881043' data-tags='AI,Science,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-science-research-flattens-discovery' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI boosts research careers but narrow the span of ideas explored: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 提升了研究者的职业前景，但也缩短了他们探索思想的广度：研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48881043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI boosts researchers&#x27; productivity and career prospects but appears to narrow the range of ideas explored, potentially flattening scientific discovery. The study suggests a trade-off between scale and novelty, underscoring the need for governance, funding diversity, and methodological safeguards to preserve exploratory research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能提升了研究者的生产力和职业前景，但似乎也缩窄了研究思路的广度，可能让科学发现的领域变得更单一。研究凸显了规模与新颖性之间的权衡，强调需要治理、资金多样性与方法论保障来保持探索性研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=62826557&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C1189%2C0%2C61' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=62826557&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C1189%2C0%2C61' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48912100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48912100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StubHub and its CEO face a class-action alleging deceptive practices related to mass scalping, alleging misrepresentation of ticket availability and pricing. The suit underscores regulatory scrutiny of secondary marketplaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StubHub 与 CEO 面临关于大规模哄抬票价的欺诈指控，指控其对票务供应与定价进行误导性宣传。此案突显对二级市场的监管关注与行业透明度议题。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48865001' data-tags='Open Source,Business,AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thoughtworks.com/insights/blog/open-source/zero-cost-fallacy-open-source-agentic-era' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The zero-cost fallacy: open-source software in the agentic era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>零成本谬误：代理时代的开源软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In an era of agentic AI, open-source software is no longer cost-free. Sustainable funding, governance, and security mechanisms are increasingly necessary as autonomous agents rely on open ecosystems. This implies a shift in how companies and communities sustain open-source projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在代理时代，开源软件已不再是零成本。随着自治代理对开放生态的依赖增加，需要可持续的资金、治理和安全机制，并推动新的开源资助与治理模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thoughtworks.com/content/dam/thoughtworks/images/illustration/brand/tw_illustration_24.jpg' data-full='https://www.thoughtworks.com/content/dam/thoughtworks/images/illustration/brand/tw_illustration_24.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48939742' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.aea9708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Helium escaping from atmosphere of nearby rocky exoplanet in a habitable zone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自近邻的岩质系外行星大气中氦气正在逸出，处于宜居带</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48939742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 146</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48939742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Helium is escaping from the atmosphere of a nearby rocky exoplanet in the habitable zone, offering clues about atmospheric loss and planetary evolution. The finding informs models of exoplanet habitability and demonstrates the power of high-resolution spectroscopy in studying small worlds. It adds a data point in understanding how rocky planets retain atmospheres.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自最近的处于宜居带的岩质系外行星大气中氦气正在逸出，为行星大气损失与演化提供线索。此发现有助于改进系外行星宜居性模型，并凸显高分辨率光谱在研究小型星球方面的能力，增加了岩质行星大气维持的实证点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A metallurgist critiques the feasibility of self-replicating probes, stressing material science and engineering constraints that challenge the idea of nanoscopic &#x27;von Neumann probes&#x27;.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位冶金学家对自我复制探针的可行性提出质疑，强调材料科学与工程约束对“冯·诺依曼探针”概念的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unseasonably warm conditions trigger banana plants to fruit in the UK after years of dormancy, highlighting how climate variability can enable surprising agricultural outcomes. The anecdote underscores evolving plant behavior in temperate regions.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.up.com/news/safety/Tracking-Rail-Heat-260608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Painting the sides of railroad rails white to reduce derailment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>铁路轨道涂白以降低热胀导致的脱轨风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48951780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48951780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Railway operator tests white-painted rail sides to reflect sunlight and reduce heat buildup, aiming to prevent rail buckling in hot weather. Early results show lower surface temperatures and fewer heat-induced derailment events, prompting wider adoption of heat mitigation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>铁路运营商通过把铁轨两边涂白来反射阳光、降低轨道温度，以减少高温下因热胀导致的脱轨风险。初步结果显示表面温度下降，热胀引发的脱轨事件减少，未来计划推广此类降温措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.up.com/content/dam/upcom/corp-comm/images/news/2026/june/IMG_UP_260608_Rail_heat.jpg' data-full='https://www.up.com/content/dam/upcom/corp-comm/images/news/2026/june/IMG_UP_260608_Rail_heat.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This paper analyzes the economic dynamics of systems that recursively improve themselves, exploring incentives, resource constraints, and potential runaway growth. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文从激励、资源约束和潜在的滚雪球式增长等角度，探讨递归自我改进系统的经济学，并分析实现路径、市场反馈以及治理与安全对相关投资的影响。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48882554' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Automation Without Understanding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需理解的自动化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 133</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Automation Without Understanding argues that autonomous systems can operate without deep human-like understanding, but this can yield brittleness and unsafe failures. The piece urges safeguards, monitoring, and clearly defined boundaries for automated agents. It underscores designing automation with verifiability and context in mind.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《无需理解的自动化》认为自动系统在缺乏深层人类式理解的情况下也能运行，但这会带来脆弱性与潜在的安全失效。文章呼吁加强安全措施、监控并为自动代理设定清晰边界，强调设计时应优先考虑可验证性与上下文。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48832329' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cwye0dlzgejo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &#x27;absolute magic&#x27; of Morse code that still connects people globally</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>摩尔斯电码的全球连接力仍在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48832329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48832329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece celebrates Morse code as a resilient, &#x27;magical&#x27; means of global communication that persists despite modern networks. It highlights its enduring relevance in emergencies, remote work, and hobbyist communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章讲述摩尔斯电码作为一种“绝对魔力”的全球通讯方式，尽管现代网络兴盛，它在紧急情况、偏远地区与爱好者社区中仍然发挥作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/7288/live/38251df0-6a5f-11f1-b7aa-49f3a4d630f8.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/7288/live/38251df0-6a5f-11f1-b7aa-49f3a4d630f8.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48896672' data-tags='Business,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joanwestenberg.com/p/the-bread-paradox-why-convenience' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bread paradox: why convenience always wins, and why SaaS isn&#x27;t doomed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面包悖论：便利为何总能取胜，以及 SaaS 并非注定失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People consistently choose the easiest, fastest path, which fuels the enduring appeal of convenient software and services. The piece argues that SaaS remains viable because frictionless onboarding, integration, and ongoing value creation outweigh concerns about consolidation, offering strong reasons for continued investment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人们总是优先选择最方便、上手最快的选项，这使得便利性成为驱动软件采用的核心力量。文章认为 SaaS 仍具生命力，因为无缝上手、良好集成和持续创造的价值，能持续让用户愿意投资。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hEI4!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F87751782-29da-46c1-a929-8b1b5ba73709_1373x1037.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!hEI4!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F87751782-29da-46c1-a929-8b1b5ba73709_1373x1037.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tokio-rs/topcoat' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Topcoat: The full full-stack framework for Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Topcoat：Rust 的全栈框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Topcoat is a full-stack web framework built for Rust, aiming to streamline front-to-back web app development with asynchronous support and integrated tooling. It competes with established Rust web ecosystems by offering cohesive routing, SSR, and API layers in one package.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Topcoat 是为 Rust 构建的全栈 Web 框架，旨在提供从前端到后端的一体化开发体系，具备异步支持和集成工具。它通过统一的路由、服务端渲染和 API 层，试图与现有 Rust Web 生态系统竞争。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b23a366da61222922366de6f685b59ce603cf9d3c6685f48efdd989a9c3fac3e/tokio-rs/topcoat' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b23a366da61222922366de6f685b59ce603cf9d3c6685f48efdd989a9c3fac3e/tokio-rs/topcoat' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Poseidon-fan/linux-0.11-rs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux 0.11 rewritten in idiomatic Rust, boots in QEMU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 0.11 用地道 Rust 重写并在 QEMU 启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48898134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48898134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An enthusiast rebuilt the Linux 0.11 kernel in idiomatic Rust and got it to boot in QEMU. The project demonstrates Rust’s viability for low-level systems work and serves as an educational bridge from classic kernels to modern tooling. It spotlights both the enduring complexity of OS internals and the expressive power of Rust abstractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人用地道的 Rust 重写 Linux 0.11，并在 QEMU 中成功启动。该项目展示了 Rust 在底层系统编程中的可行性，并为将经典内核迁移到现代工具链提供了学习路径。它揭示了操作系统内部的复杂性，以及 Rust 抽象能力的强大。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Orion Browser is a privacy-focused browser from Kagi that emphasizes tracker blocking and transparent privacy controls. It adds to a growing landscape of privacy-first browsers, signaling demand for a more private web experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Orion 浏览器由 Kagi 打造，主打隐私保护，强化对追踪器的屏蔽并提供透明的隐私控制。此类隐私优先浏览器的崛起，反映出人们对更私密网页体验的需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://typesanitizer.com/blog/job-queues.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Job queues are deceptively tricky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>作业队列其实很棘手</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Job queues present subtle pitfalls: scheduling, retries, backpressure, and fairness can drastically affect throughput and latency in distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作业队列在分布式系统中隐藏着棘手的坑点，调度、重试、回压和公平性等因素会显著影响吞吐量和延迟。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://app.everything.diena.co/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A zoomable timeline of 4M Wikipedia events</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示一个可缩放的 400 万条维基百科事件时间线，支持多尺度浏览历史数据。该项目有助于研究者和知识图谱，将事件与主题、地点和时间连接起来。</p>
