<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.motivenotes.ai/p/against-usefulness' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Against Usefulness</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对实用性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Against Usefulness argues that usefulness alone is an insufficient metric for evaluating technology; it advocates broader criteria like resilience, beauty, and social impact. The piece challenges builders to consider long-term trade-offs and unintended consequences. It proposes a more nuanced framework for value assessment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《反对实用性》认为仅以实用性作为评估技术的唯一标准不足以全面衡量价值，倡导将韧性、美感与社会影响等纳入评估。文章质疑开发者在设计时对长期取舍与潜在后果的忽视，并提出更为细致的价值评估框架。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Maya inscriptions from Guatemala identify an ancient astronomer, shedding light on how Maya scholars tracked celestial cycles and integrated astronomy into daily life. The finding reframes our understanding of pre-Columbian science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>危地马拉玛雅遗址的新文本揭示了一位古代天文学家，表明玛雅学者对天文现象与日常生活的紧密结合，重新塑造了对前哥伦布时期科学的认识。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryanjk5.github.io/posts/rjk-duck/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Beautiful Type Erasure with C++26 Reflection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 C++26 反射实现优雅的类型擦除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C++26 introduces reflection features that simplify type erasure, letting library authors write generic interfaces with less boilerplate and zero-cost abstractions. The piece demonstrates elegant patterns for erasing concrete types using reflection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C++26 引入的反射特性简化了类型擦除，使库作者能够用更少的样板代码实现通用接口，且保持零代价抽象。文章演示了用反射实现类型擦除的优雅模式。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A minuscule cell challenges a fundamental rule of the nitrogen cycle, hinting at alternative metabolic pathways and microbial processes. The finding could influence fertilizer efficiency, bioprocessing, and our understanding of microbial nitrogen transformations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现一种微小细胞挑战了氮循环中的基本规律，暴露出可能的替代代谢路径。此发现或影响肥料效率、生物工艺及对微生物氮转化过程的理解。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eetimes.com/risc-v-is-inevitable-state-of-the-union-keynote-argues/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RISC-V Is Inevitable: State of the Union Keynote Argues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RISC-V 必然到来：年度总结主旨演讲提出的论点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open, extensible ISA is gaining momentum across microcontrollers to servers, and a recent keynote argues that RISC-V&#x27;s trajectory is unstoppable. The presentation highlights ecosystem growth, industry adoption, and policy interest, while acknowledging tooling and performance parity gaps that must be closed for broad replacement of incumbents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源、可定制的 RISC-V 正在从嵌入式走向更广泛的生态。主旨演讲强调生态扩张、产业采纳和政策推动，但也点出工具链成熟度与性能对标等挑战，若要替代现有架构还需继续完善。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>REO Trucks introduces the I4 4WD pickup with a starting price of $21,500, targeting budget-minded buyers. The entry price underscores the market’s demand for affordable, capable work trucks and could pressure incumbents to rethink value in the segment. This launch signals continued competition in the low-cost pickup space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>REO Trucks 推出 I4 4WD 皮卡，起价为$21,500，瞄准预算有限的买家。这个定价凸显市场对价格友好、实用皮卡的需求，可能促使传统厂商重新思考低端价值。该产品进入低成本皮卡市场，意味着竞争将更激烈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://reotrucks.com/opengraph-image?2c37889503466c53' data-full='https://reotrucks.com/opengraph-image?2c37889503466c53' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vshulcz/deja-vu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open-source memory for coding agents, synced over SSH</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于编码代理的开源记忆，支持通过 SSH 同步</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48923111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48923111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source memory system for coding agents that can sync over SSH. It aims to give AI agents persistent context across sessions and machines, improving collaboration and reliability in coding tasks. The SSH syncing hints at secure, lightweight deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个面向编码代理的开源记忆系统，能够通过 SSH 同步实现跨会话、跨机器的上下文持久化。它改善了在编码任务中的协作性与稳定性，并且强调了安全、轻量级的部署方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b3d1a0142c7409bd686cae4d5decf257442bd0755654b6de19a4159177090c16/vshulcz/deja-vu' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b3d1a0142c7409bd686cae4d5decf257442bd0755654b6de19a4159177090c16/vshulcz/deja-vu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48918575' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/articles/llm-and-dsls.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DSLs Enable Reliable Use of LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DSLs 使得大语言模型的使用更可靠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48918575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48918575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that DSLs can constrain LLM behavior and improve reliability, offering explicit structures for prompts, validation, and integration patterns. It provides examples for building DSLs around data querying, code generation, or API usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DSLs 能提高对大语言模型的使用可靠性，提供提示、校验与集成的结构化方法，文中给出数据查询、代码生成等场景的示例。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rebeccamakkai.substack.com/p/book-prizes-dont-work-how-you-think' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Book prizes don&#x27;t work how you think</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>书奖并不像你想象的那样有效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48913653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Book prizes don&#x27;t work as you think; committees&#x27; biases and symbolic value affect publishing decisions beyond prize money. The piece analyzes incentives and systemic effects on authors, publishers, and readers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>书籍奖项的运作并非你想象中那样简单，评审偏好与象征性价值会影响出版生态，远超奖金本身。文章分析了激励机制及对作者、出版商和读者的系统性影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/marcelpanse/youtube-guitar-tab-parser' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: YouTube Guitar Tab Parser</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48898154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48898154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source YouTube guitar tab parser that extracts and structures guitar tablature from video content. It showcases how media content can be transformed into reusable, machine-readable data for musicians and educators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个开源的 YouTube 吉他谱解析器，能够从视频中提取并结构化吉他谱数据。它演示了如何把多媒体内容转化为可重复使用、机器可读的数据，便于音乐家和教育者使用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48883090' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/news/can-we-understand-how-large-language-models-reason/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mechanistic interpretability researchers applying causality theory to LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机制性可解释性研究者把因果理论应用于大语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mechanistic interpretability researchers are applying causality theory to LLMs to uncover causal structures behind reasoning and behavior. This shift aims to move beyond correlations toward interpretable cause-effect dynamics, aiding debugging and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>机制性可解释性研究者正在把因果理论引入大语言模型，试图揭示模型推理和行为背后的因果结构。目标是从单纯相关性转向可因果推理的解释框架，帮助调试和提升安全性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Latent space—the hidden structure learned by generative models—constitutes a new medium for art and interaction, shaping how we create and experience AI-powered content. It discusses implications for design, collaboration, and media futures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将潜在空间视作新媒介，讨论生成模型学习的隐藏结构如何成为创作与互动的新途径，影响我们对 AI 内容的设计与体验。探讨对设计、协作与媒体未来的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Schema Harness 在 Arc-AGI-3 公共基准上约获 99% 的表现，显示基于模式推理系统的强劲性能，并为数据驱动 AI 的扩展性讨论提供新证据。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipsandcheese.com/p/is-x86-ready-to-ace-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is x86 ready to ACE it?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>x86 是否准备好大显身手？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece evaluates whether x86 architecture is keeping pace with AI workloads and advanced accelerators, considering performance, memory bandwidth, and compiler ecosystem. It discusses potential pathways for x86 to stay relevant in AI era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章评估 x86 架构是否能跟上 AI 工作负载与高级加速器的需求，涉及性能、内存带宽和编译器生态等因素，并探讨 x86 在 AI 时代保持相关性的可能路径。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Danau5tin/ai-trains-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I RL-trained an agent that trains models with RL (for ~$1.3k)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我用 RL 训练了一个用 RL 训练模型的代理（花费约 1.3 千美元）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proof-of-concept agent trained with reinforcement learning uses RL to train other models, achieving a full loop on AI training at a modest budget.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用强化学习训练、再用强化学习训练其他模型的代理，展示了以低成本完成整套 AI 训练循环的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/a-philosophers-one-word-theory-to' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agnes Callard’s theory of the uni-context</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿格尼斯·卡拉德的统一情境理论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Philosopher Agnes Callard proposes a &#x27;uni-context&#x27; approach, arguing that meaning hinges on a single, shared frame. This lens challenges pluralistic interpretations and invites reflection on how we compare normative arguments across cultures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>哲学家阿格尼斯·卡拉德提出了“统一情境”观念，认为意义依赖于一个共同的框架。这一视角挑战了多元化解读，促使我们反思在跨文化讨论中如何比较规范性论点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1759298928528-68604ad099f5?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wzMDAzMzh8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwyfHxhYnN0cmFjdCUyMGRpcmVjdGlvbnxlbnwwfHx8fDE3ODM5NDY0NTJ8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1759298928528-68604ad099f5?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wzMDAzMzh8MHwxfHNlYXJjaHwyfHxhYnN0cmFjdCUyMGRpcmVjdGlvbnxlbnwwfHx8fDE3ODM5NDY0NTJ8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。若合并，将简化编译器维护并加速对新目标的尝试。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thinkingmachines.ai/inkling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Murati&#x27;s Thinking Machines Releases Open-Weights 975B Parameter LLM</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48924929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48924929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thinking Machines releases an open-weights LLM at 975B parameters, marking a bold step toward accessible, large-scale AI research. Public weights fuel community benchmarking and collaboration, but also raise questions about safety, licensing, and guardrails. This could shift who can responsibly experiment with models of that scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Thinking Machines 推出 9750 亿参数的开源权重大模型，标志着向可访问的大规模 AI 研究迈出关键一步。公开权重促进社区基准测试与协作，但也带来安全、许可和治理等新问题，或影响谁能以负责任的方式研究如此规模的模型。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wikipedia narrowly avoids Category 1 designation under the UK Online Safety Act for now, leaving questions about moderation obligations unresolved. The piece outlines potential future compliance implications for editors and hosting platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维基百科在英国 Online Safety Act 下暂时逃离 Category 1 的 designation，对编辑与托管平台的未来合规影响尚不明朗。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48894630' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jbwinters/jacquard-lang' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Jacquard, a programming language for AI-written, human-reviewed code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Jacquard，一种用于 AI 生成、人工审核代码的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jacquard proposes a programming language designed for AI-generated code that is nonetheless reviewed by humans. It explores tooling for traceability, reproducibility, and safe AI-assisted software development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jacquard 设计了一种用于 AI 生成、由人类审核代码的编程语言，强调可追溯性与可审查性。它探索了在 AI 辅助编程中实现更安全、可控的软件开发工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/144ccf12fbf5f800ffe4c82df8e5257dc933cb4543711269681d0ad7bd2cf997/jbwinters/jacquard-lang' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/144ccf12fbf5f800ffe4c82df8e5257dc933cb4543711269681d0ad7bd2cf997/jbwinters/jacquard-lang' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48853146' data-tags='Science,Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/world-first-super-alloy-could-transform-the-way-metals-are-made' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>World-First &#x27;Super Alloy&#x27; Could Transform the Way Metals Are Made</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球首个‘超合金’或将改变金属制造方式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48853146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48853146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A breakthrough in metallurgical materials—a &#x27;super alloy&#x27; with exceptional strength and heat resistance—promises to transform metal production and component performance. If scalable, it could cut energy use and enable longer-lasting components in aerospace, power, and manufacturing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种具有卓越强度与耐热性的“超合金”实现材料科学的突破，可能改变金属生产与部件性能的格局。若实现规模化，将降低能源消耗，推动航空、能源与制造等领域的耐用部件应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/07/alloy-closeup.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/07/alloy-closeup.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qubes OS Security in the Public Record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>公开记录中的 Qubes OS 安全性</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of Qubes OS security claims based on public records, evaluating how well isolation holds up under practical threat models. The review highlights strengths and remaining gaps, informing users and researchers about practical security expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于公开记录对 Qubes OS 安全性的声称进行评估，检验隔离在实际威胁模型下的稳健性。讨论指出优点与潜在漏洞，为用户与研究者提供实际的安全期望与改进方向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://psychedelics.community/cultural-icons/terence-mckennas-mega-bad-trip' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence McKenna&#x27;s Mega Bad Trip (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特伦斯·麦肯纳的 Mega Bad Trip</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece revisits Terence McKenna&#x27;s infamous &#x27;Mega Bad Trip&#x27; and its lasting impact on psychedelic discourse and pop culture around his ideas. It illustrates how intense psychedelic experiences shape narratives and reputations in subcultures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾了特伦斯·麦肯纳著名的“Mega Bad Trip”以及它对迷幻文化和他思想公众形象的持续影响。内容揭示了强烈的迷幻体验如何塑造子文化中的叙事与声誉。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/guardian-angel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Guardian Angels: LLM Personalization for Productivity and Security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>守护天使：提升生产力与安全性的LLM个性化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48906041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48906041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Personalized LLMs can boost productivity while embedding guardrails to protect privacy and security. The piece outlines approaches like on-device customization, user-controlled data sharing, and governance to balance convenience with risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个性化的LLM能提升工作效率，同时嵌入守护机制以保护隐私与安全。文中提出本地化定制、用户可控的数据共享与治理等方法，在便利性与风险之间寻求平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minikotlin presents a compact, lightweight Kotlin subset or runtime, useful for embedding Kotlin-like semantics in constrained environments. It illustrates how language design trade-offs enable smaller footprints while retaining familiar syntax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Minikotlin 展示了一个极简的 Kotlin 子集/运行时，适合在资源受限环境中嵌入 Kotlin 风格的语义。它展示了语言设计权衡如何在保持熟悉语法的同时实现更小的体积。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://om-intelligence.ch/projects/vocal-notation/vocal-notation.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn your singing voice into printable notes (in the browser)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在浏览器中将你的歌声转为可打印的乐谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48900686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48900686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-browser tool that converts singing voice into musical notation using pitch tracking and timing extraction to produce printable scores. Demonstrates accessible, client-side audio processing for creative workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器中运行的工具，通过音高跟踪与节拍提取将歌声转写为可打印的乐谱，展现了面向创作的前端音频处理能力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agnost AI 提供从代理对话中提取结构化用户反馈的工具，帮助产品团队更高效地迭代并改进代理表现，提升迭代效率。它将对话中的反馈转化为可操作的改进点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48971418' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dornsife.usc.edu/news/stories/heavy-tv-watching-associated-with-smaller-brain-structures/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Heavy TV watching associated with smaller brain structures, study finds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大量看电视与脑部结构变小相关，研究发现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48971418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A USC Dornsife study links heavy television viewing with reductions in certain brain structures, suggesting lifestyle factors may correlate with brain morphology. While not proof of causation, it adds to evidence that prolonged screen time could relate to structural differences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>南加州大学蒙相关研究发现，长期大量观看电视与一些脑部结构变小相关，提示生活方式与脑形态之间存在关联。尽管不能证明因果，但该研究为屏幕使用时长与脑结构变化之间的关系提供了证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dornsife.usc.edu/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/7/2026/06/raichlen-sedentary-brain-top.jpg' data-full='https://dornsife.usc.edu/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/7/2026/06/raichlen-sedentary-brain-top.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48964536' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fda.gov/news-events/press-announcements/fda-approves-first-oral-pcsk9-inhibitor-lower-ldl-cholesterol-adults-high-cholesterol' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FDA approves new kind of cholesterol pill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FDA 批准首款口服 PCSK9 抑制剂降胆固醇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FDA has approved the first oral PCSK9 inhibitor to lower LDL cholesterol in adults with high cholesterol. This pill could broaden access to PCSK9 therapy by offering a convenient alternative to injections, though questions remain about long-term safety and cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FDA 批准了首款口服 PCSK9 抑制剂，用于降低高胆固醇成人的 LDL 水平。这一药物可能通过提供注射药物的便捷替代方案来扩展治疗覆盖面，但关于长期安全性与成本仍存疑问。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fda.gov/themes/custom/preview/img/FDA-Social-Graphic.png' data-full='https://www.fda.gov/themes/custom/preview/img/FDA-Social-Graphic.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48852151' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/security/application-security/how-github-gave-every-repository-a-durable-owner/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How GitHub gave every repository a durable owner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 如何为每个仓库确立长期所有者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48852151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48852151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub introduced a durable owner model to ensure repositories always have a reliable owner, even as teams reorganize. The change improves security and governance by preventing ownership drift and orphaned repositories.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 引入一种确保每个仓库始终拥有稳定所有者的机制，即使团队重组也不易丢失所有权。此举通过防止所有权漂移和仓库无人接管来提升安全性与治理水平。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-invertocat-mona-logo.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-invertocat-mona-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bruceediger.com/posts/tftp-honeypot-results/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TFTP Honey Pot Results</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TFTP 蜜罐结果</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A TFTP honeypot study reveals attacker behaviors and common exploitation patterns against TFTP services. Findings emphasize misconfigurations to watch and the need for better monitoring of legacy protocols.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 TFTP 蜜罐的研究揭示了攻击者的行为模式与常见利用方式。研究强调对旧式协议的错误配置和监控需求，以及加强对这类协议的防护。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出代理性循环模型，描述决策、行动与学习三个互相作用的反馈环路及其安全与治理挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Co-evolution of self-replication and function in a digital primordial soup</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数字原始汤中自我复制与功能的共同进化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study showing how self-replication and functional complexity co-evolve in a digital primordial soup, shedding light on how life-like behavior can emerge in artificial systems. The results inform both artificial life research and algorithm design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究顯示在數字原始湯中，自我複製與功能複雜性共同進化，揭示了類生命行為如何在人工系統中出現。結論為人工生命研究與演算法設計提供新見解。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frank.computer/blog/2025/05/just-a-tool.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop saying that AI is just a tool and it only matters how it is used</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI is not a neutral instrument: its capabilities, biases, and governance implications shape outcomes beyond user intent. It calls for stronger framing, policy, and responsible design beyond usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强调 AI 不应被视作单纯的工具，其能力、偏见与治理框架会塑造结果，因此需要对设计、政策与责任进行更深层的考虑。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48959297' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/blog/our-approach-to-bioresilience/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our Approach to Bioresilience: Isomorphic Labs and Google DeepMind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生物韧性之路：Isomorphic Labs 与 Google DeepMind 的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48959297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48959297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Isomorphic Labs and Google DeepMind outline a joint approach to bioresilience, blending AI with wet-lab biology to accelerate discovery while prioritizing safety and governance. They discuss scalable AI models, modular workflows, and responsible research practices to reduce risk in biotech innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Isomorphic Labs 与 Google DeepMind 共同提出面向生物韧性的研发路线，将 AI 与湿实验室生物学相结合，以加速创新并优先考虑安全与治理。文章讨论可扩展的 AI 模型、模块化工作流，以及在生物技术研究中降低风险的负责任实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/ZRyQg91pjea8kXXaGSlnZIJF90-VwnKHSBxo22e2RedCvCCszsurR8W9NHw80e1V6HtWXccbU1MI27Rtjjf963rD673XgkWK7cZe_1ekfruPgdHHVEc=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/ZRyQg91pjea8kXXaGSlnZIJF90-VwnKHSBxo22e2RedCvCCszsurR8W9NHw80e1V6HtWXccbU1MI27Rtjjf963rD673XgkWK7cZe_1ekfruPgdHHVEc=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can LLMs Perform Deep Technical Comprehension of Computer Architecture Papers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大语言模型能否深入理解计算机体系结构论文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48929660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48929660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper examines whether LLMs can truly understand dense architecture research, testing their ability to parse methods, results, and diagrams. It surfaces current limitations and hints at how benchmarks and prompting techniques shape what &#x27;understanding&#x27; means for AI models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文评估大语言模型是否真的能深入理解计算机体系结构论文，分析它们在方法、结果与图表方面的理解能力及局限。提出需要的基准和提示策略来界定 AI 的“理解”边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sofar.belfortlabs.cloud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Homomorphically encrypted CIFAR-10 inference in 200ms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>同态加密 CIFAR-10 推理在 200ms</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48949240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48949240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates a CIFAR-10 inference using homomorphic encryption at around 200 milliseconds, marking progress toward privacy-preserving ML that runs on encrypted data. The result reflects advances in cryptography-accelerated AI and ongoing performance challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示在同态加密下对 CIFAR-10 的推理约在 200 毫秒完成，显示出在加密数据上进行隐私保护机器学习的进展。此成果体现了结合密码学与 AI 的进展以及仍待突破的性能挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.isomorphiclabs.com/articles/the-isomorphic-labs-drug-design-engine-unlocks-a-new-frontier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Isomorphic Labs Drug Design Engine unlocks a new frontier beyond AlphaFold</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Isomorphic Labs 的药物设计引擎开启 AlphaFold 之外的新前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Isomorphic Labs’ drug design engine aims to accelerate discovery beyond AlphaFold, combining AI with physics-based modeling to guide molecule design. The approach signals a shift from structure prediction toward generative design and optimization at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Isomorphic Labs 的药物设计引擎旨在通过将人工智能与物理建模结合，推动药物发现进入 AlphaFold 之外的新阶段，聚焦分子设计的生成与大规模优化。此举标志着从结构预测向生成设计的转变。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://minimaxir.com/2026/07/agent-quota-reset/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What&#x27;s the deal with all the random weekly quota resets for agents lately?</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post investigates why some AI agent platforms reset quotas on a weekly basis at seemingly random times, discussing implications for calibration, experimentation, and reproducibility. It explores potential bugs or design choices and offers tips for developers to manage quotas or plan around resets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析了一些 AI Agent 平台为何以看似随机的时间每周重置配额，对实验可重复性和调参造成影响。探讨了可能的漏洞或设计取舍，并给开发者在应对重置时的策略与建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/networking/clever-hacker-fits-537-000-domains-in-a-tiny-usd5-esp32-ad-blocking-dongle-firmware-uses-only-around-50kb-of-ram-and-can-answer-blocked-lookups-in-10-milliseconds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clever hacker fits 537,000 domains in a $5 ESP32 ad-blocking dongle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巧妙黑客用 5 美元 ESP32 广告拦截 dongle 容纳 537,000 域名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clever hack fits 537,000 domain entries into a ~$5 ESP32-based ad-blocking dongle, with around 50KB RAM usage and near-instant lookups. It demonstrates how small devices can perform substantial DNS-like filtering with efficient data structures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名巧妙的黑客将 537,000 条域名信息塞进约 5 美元的 ESP32 广告拦截 dongle，RAM 约 50KB，查询在 10 毫秒级。此举展示了小型设备也能通过高效数据结构实现大规模的域名过滤。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diophantine equations illustrate deep links between number theory and computation, with implications for cryptography and algorithm design. Studying them sharpens problem-solving instincts and provides transferable mathematical tools, aided by modular techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丢番图方程揭示数论与计算之间的深层联系，对密码学与算法设计具有重要影响。研究它们能磨炼解决问题的直觉，并提供可迁移的数学工具，尤其在模方法等技术方面。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48875188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48875188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Andon is a signaling system used in manufacturing to alert supervisors of problems and halt production when quality or process issues arise. It enables frontline workers to trigger quick interventions, reducing downtime and waste; modern implementations integrate with digital dashboards and escalation workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安灯是一套在生产现场用来发出信号、通知问题并在必要时叫停生产的系统。它让一线员工能迅速干预，显著降低停机和浪费，现代实现往往与数字看板和升级流程相结合。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A newly released IA-64 emulator can boot Windows, enabling enthusiasts to run Itanium-era software on modern hardware through full-system emulation. The project highlights long-standing emulation challenges and the persistence of legacy ecosystems.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/07/20/when-ai-is-a-member-of-the-family' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When A.I. is a member of the family</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当 AI 成为家庭成员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A meditation on how AI could become part of family life, exploring emotional bonds, caregiving roles, and the ethical boundary between humans and machines. The piece probes long-term social and psychological implications as AI becomes more present.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于 AI 可能成为家庭成员的反思，探讨情感联系、看护角色以及人机界限的伦理边界。文章关注 AI 日益进入日常生活对社会与心理层面的长远影响。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48862351' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://utcc.utoronto.ca/~cks/space/blog/sysadmin/DHCPServerAndScreamingHost' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An unusual way for your DHCP server to run out of dynamic IPs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DHCP 服务器用一种不寻常的方式耗尽动态 IP</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48862351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog explores an edge case where a DHCP server exhausts its dynamic IP pool in an unusual way (e.g., due to lease misconfiguration or pathological client behavior). The example underscores the importance of sane lease lifetimes, pool sizing, and monitoring to avoid network access issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博客介绍一个罕见的 DHCP 池耗尽案例，可能由租期设置不当或异常客户端行为引发。该案例强调配置合理的租期、池容量和监控的必要性，以避免网络访问中断。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/CharmingBlaze/moonbasic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MoonBASIC: A modern BASIC for building 2D and 3D games</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MoonBASIC is a modern take on BASIC designed for building 2D and 3D games, blending approachable syntax with contemporary tooling. It illustrates how retro-inspired languages can evolve for modern game development and education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MoonBASIC 是对 BASIC 的现代化演绎，面向 2D/3D 游戏开发，结合易用语法与现代工具链。它展示了复古风格的语言如何进化以用于现代游戏开发与教育。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48906171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48906171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Igalia reports on WebKit’s Layer Based SVG Engine, detailing how conditional layers reduce overhead and improve rendering performance for complex SVGs. The update reflects ongoing browser rendering optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Igalia 介绍了 WebKit 的基于图层的 SVG 引擎，说明条件图层如何降低开销并提升复杂 SVG 的渲染性能。更新体现了浏览器渲染优化的持续进展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boilingsteam.com/steam-machine-between-10k-and-15k-sold-per-week/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steam Machine: Between 12k and 15k Units Sold per week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steam Machine：每周销量在 1.2 万至 1.5 万台之间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steam Machine is reportedly moving 12k–15k units weekly, indicating a modest but steady demand for Valve&#x27;s hardware initiative, despite tough competition in the living room and PC gaming. The numbers offer clues about hardware adoption, supply chain stability, and the broader health of the Steam ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Steam Machine 的周销量约为 1.2万至 1.5万台，显示 Valve 硬件计划在激烈的家用娱乐与 PC 游戏市场竞争中的相对稳定需求。此数据可揭示硬件采用、供应链稳健性以及 Steam 生态的整体健康状况。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48917055' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(363) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.apnic.net/2026/07/15/whos-running-all-those-tiny-rpki-servers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who&#x27;s running all those tiny RPKI servers?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>是谁在运营那些微型 RPKI 服务器？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many small validators operate RPKI services, contributing to routing security by signing route-origin attestations, but their scale and maintenance often lag larger operators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大量微型 RPKI 验证节点在运行，它们为路由安全贡献签署路由原点证明，但规模和运维往往不如大厂。文章呼吁改进监控、标准和治理，确保关键互联网基础设施的可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/routing-ft.jpg' data-full='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/routing-ft.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google outlines emoji design updates aligned with how we communicate today, including more nuanced, accessible, and platform-consistent visuals. The work aims to reflect real usage, cultural nuance, and identity in emoji sets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌介绍了与当代沟通方式相匹配的表情符号设计更新，强调更丰富的表达、可访问性与跨平台一致性。该工作力求通过考虑使用场景、文化差异与身份认同来优化表情符号集合。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tracing Starlink-like ideas back to 1984, this piece situates the modern satellite internet project in a longer history of global-connectivity concepts, highlighting how feasibility, cost, and policy shape deployment.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A paper argues coding agents can plan ahead by simulating future states, improving reliability. The approach integrates forward-looking reasoning into automated coding workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出编码代理通过模拟未来状态来进行前瞻性规划，从而提升可靠性。该方法将前瞻性推理融入自动化编码工作流。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeBSD 16 的基系统核心组件逐渐摆脱 GPL 代码，标志着许可证变更对核心组件分发与再利用的影响。此举会影响许可合规和下游项目的兼容性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipsandcheese.com/p/scrying-the-amd-gfx1250-llvm-tea' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scrying the AMD GFX1250 LLVM Tea Leaves</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48965161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chips and Cheese digs into LLVM back-end clues for AMD&#x27;s GFX1250, piecing together speculative details about the GPU&#x27;s architecture and compiler support. The piece helps readers understand how compiler tooling leaks can reveal upcoming hardware capabilities before official disclosures, and why that matters for performance and driver development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chips and Cheese 根据 LLVM 的线索猜测 AMD GFX1250 的架构与编译器支持，试图在官方披露前拼出潜在性能特征。文章揭示了编译工具链的线索如何让外界提前洞察新一代显卡的能力，以及这对性能优化和驱动开发的意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sph.unc.edu/sph-news/no-link-between-acetaminophen-use-during-pregnancy-and-adverse-birth-outcomes-study-finds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No link between acetaminophen use during pregnancy and adverse birth outcomes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>孕期使用对乙酰氨基酚与不良出生结局无显著相关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48959302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48959302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new study finds no link between acetaminophen use during pregnancy and adverse birth outcomes, easing concerns for expectant parents and clinicians.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示孕期使用对乙酰氨基酚与不良出生结局之间无显著相关，为准父母与医生带来安慰。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48942603' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://in-toto.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In-toto: A framework to secure the integrity of software supply chains</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>In-toto：保障软件供应链完整性的框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48942603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48942603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In-toto offers a framework for end-to-end attestation and verification of software supply chains, enabling organizations to specify expected build steps and materials. By standardizing how provenance is recorded and checked, it helps detect tampering and ensure trust from source to deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>In-toto 提供一个端到端的软件供应链可证明性与验证框架，允许组织规定期望的构建步骤和材料。通过对来源与构建过程进行标准化记录与校验，它有助于发现篡改并提升部署前后的信任。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/epdf/10.1145/36177.36194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superoptimizer – A Look at the Smallest Program (1987) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超级优化器：最小程序的研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Superoptimizer explores the smallest possible program that still accomplishes a task, challenging assumptions about compiler optimization and program synthesis. It surveys the limits of program compression and has implications for verification and teaching about compilers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>超级优化器研究在完成任务的前提下可达到的最小程序规模，挑战了对编译器优化与程序综合的直觉。它通过寻找最小指令序列来实现正确性，影响我们对优化、验证与编译教育的理解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这台全球最大的概率性计算机利用随机性从嘈杂数据中提取有用信息，展示了基于随机性的计算如何补充传统 AI 负载并提升鲁棒性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shub.club/writings/2026/july/check-your-scrollheight/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are you telling me a readonly property is wrecking my performance?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>只读属性真能拖慢性能吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A performance note on how reading a read-only property can trigger layout recalculations in web apps, potentially thrashing scroll performance. The takeaway is to minimize synchronous reads in hot paths and to batch DOM reads with writes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲解在网页应用中读取只读属性可能触发浏览器布局计算，从而降低滚动性能。建议在热路径中尽量减少同步读取，并将 DOM 读取与写入分批处理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shub.club/img/me-today-small-25w.jpeg' data-full='https://shub.club/img/me-today-small-25w.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48969582' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thedrive.com/news/how-texas-police-spent-4-5-million-on-four-chevy-tahoes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Texas Police Spent $4.5M on Four Chevy Tahoes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德州警察花费 450 万美元购买四辆 Chevy Tahoe</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48969582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at a large spending on just four vehicles by Texas police, raising questions about procurement efficiency and cost control in public safety fleets. The piece prompts reflection on budgeting, maintenance, and transparency in government purchases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道聚焦德州警方在四辆 Chevy Tahoe 上耗资约 450 万美元的采购，质疑公共安全车队的采购效率与成本控制。文章引发对政府采购透明度、维护成本和预算分配的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thedrive.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/police-tahoe.jpg?w=1200' data-full='https://www.thedrive.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/police-tahoe.jpg?w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.onepercentbrighter.com/p/natural-experiments-prove-feeding' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Natural experiments prove phytoplankton carbon removal works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自然实验证明浮游植物碳去除有效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Natural experiments provide evidence that phytoplankton-driven carbon removal works, suggesting a viable nature-based approach to climate mitigation. The findings support pursuing ocean-based carbon sequestration while noting uncertainties around scale and ecological side effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自然实验显示浮游植物通过光合作用实现碳去除，支持以海洋为基础的气候缓解策略。研究强调海洋碳汇的潜力，但在规模化、成本与生态副作用方面仍存在不确定性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>N+1</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48851412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48851412' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>N+1 examines the idea of adding one more element to a system and the implications of that marginal change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>N+1 探讨在系统中再增加一个元素的想法及其边际变化带来的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c4gyg0y6yg2o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teen hackers who live streamed cyber-attack on TfL jailed</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two teenage hackers who livestreamed a cyber-attack on Transport for London were jailed. The case underscores the seriousness of cybercrime and the consequences of online public wrongdoing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两名青少年黑客因对伦敦交通局发动网络攻击并进行直播而被判刑。此案凸显网络犯罪的严重性以及在网上公开作恶的后果。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.capitalone.com/tech/open-source/announcing-vulnhunter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VulnHunter: Capital One&#x27;s agentic AI code security tool</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Capital One launches VulnHunter, an AI-driven tool that autonomously scans code for vulnerabilities, signaling a shift toward proactive, agentic security tooling. The approach could accelerate secure software delivery while raising questions about auditability and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Capital One 推出 VulnHunter，一款能够自主扫描代码漏洞的 AI 驱动工具，标志着对主动化安全工具的推进。此举有望加速安全软件交付，但也对可审计性与安全性提出新挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/hgaiser/moonshine' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moonshine: Lets you stream games from your PC to any device running Moonlight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moonshine：让你把 PC 游戏流式传输到任何运行 Moonlight 的设备</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48972970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48972970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moonshine is a tool that enables streaming PC games to devices running Moonlight, expanding game streaming beyond official options. It highlights the ongoing ecosystem around Moonlight/GameStream, offering flexibility for multi-device play.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moonshine 允许把电脑上的游戏流式传输到支持 Moonlight 的设备，拓展了官方 GameStream 的场景。此工具彰显了 Moonlight/GameStream 生态的活跃性，为跨设备低延迟游戏体验提供新的可能。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48850688' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lamport.azurewebsites.net/pubs/paxos-simple.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Paxos Made Simple (2001)[pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Paxos 简明论点（2001）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48850688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48850688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lamport&#x27;s Paxos presented a concise, practical formulation of a distributed consensus algorithm; the paper remains foundational for building reliable fault-tolerant systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lamport 的 Paxos 以简洁、务实的方式给出了分布式一致性算法的核心思想，至今是构建容错系统的基础性文献。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48929587' data-tags='Programming,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pmbanugo.me/blog/why-async-await-complect-concurrency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Tokio/Rayon Trap and Why Async/Await Fails Concurrency</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tokio/Rayon 陷阱，以及为何 Async/Await 不能实现并发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48929587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48929587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that combining Tokio and Rayon can create subtleties where async/await mismanages CPU-bound workloads, revealing traps in concurrency design. It suggests patterns to avoid these pitfalls and to better exploit parallelism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探索 Tokio 与 Rayon 的协作陷阱，指出异步/等待模型在处理 CPU 绑定工作时可能导致的并发问题。给出避免这些坑的方法与对并发设计的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dev-to-uploads.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/articles/kgd6qp4xhkmjmj721nq4.png' data-full='https://dev-to-uploads.s3.amazonaws.com/uploads/articles/kgd6qp4xhkmjmj721nq4.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openthemagazine.com/india/the-second-life-of-sanskrit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Second Life of Sanskrit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>梵语的第二次生命</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sanskrit is seeing renewed interest beyond academia, with modern curricula, digital resources, and translation work expanding its relevance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梵语在学术圈之外重新获得关注，教育课程、数字资源和翻译工作正扩展其现代意义。文章探讨在教育与科技领域重燃梵语热潮的努力，呈现一种更广义的语言复兴。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tug.org/tug2026/preprints/lode-realtime.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Real-Time LuaTeX: Recompiling Large Documents in 1ms [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>实时 LuaTeX：大文档可在 1 ms 内重新编译</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A preprint describing techniques for real-time LuaTeX incremental recompilation, enabling near-instant feedback when editing large LaTeX documents. The work showcases caching and dependency-tracking improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>预印本介绍了 LuaTeX 的实时增量重新编译技术，在编辑大文档时实现近乎瞬时的反馈。该工作展示了缓存和依赖追踪等改进。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48896818' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.thunderbird.net/2026/07/desktopsettings/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thunderbird Desktop settings research: what we learned from your feedback</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Thunderbird 桌面设置调研：从用户反馈中学到的经验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thunderbird published insights from a desktop settings study based on user feedback, outlining UX improvements, configuration challenges, and guidance for future refinements. The findings guide practical tweaks for usability and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Thunderbird 基于用户反馈总结桌面设置的改进要点，提出了设计与配置方面的关键教训，帮助规划未来的改进方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.thunderbird.net/files/2026/07/blog-banner-generic1.jpg' data-full='https://blog.thunderbird.net/files/2026/07/blog-banner-generic1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cdrom.ca/games/2026/05/30/xd.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Trip to 90s Kansai: Exploring the XD FirstClass Network BBS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>90年代关西之旅：探索 XD FirstClass BBS 网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nostalgic account of the XD FirstClass BBS network in 1990s Kansai, exploring the communities and tech that shaped early online culture. The piece shows how dial-up days fostered collaboration before the web exploded.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 1990 年代关西地区的 XD FirstClass BBS 网络，介绍其社区、软件和在线文化，对比现代网络生态。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48889148' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/quadrupling-code-performance-with-a-useless-if/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quadrupling code performance with a &quot;useless&quot; if</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用一个“无用的 if” 将代码性能提升四倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A micro-optimization showing that an apparently useless-if condition can significantly boost performance by shaping CPU branch prediction and cache behavior. The piece cautions about hardware-specific tricks and the risk of over-optimizing for edge cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了一种微优化：一个看似无用的 if 条件却通过影响分支预测和缓存行为显著提升性能。文章提醒读者警惕针对特定硬件的优化，避免过度优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/quadrupling-code-performance-with-a-useless-if/og.png' data-full='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/quadrupling-code-performance-with-a-useless-if/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
