<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/LazoVelko/neverclick' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neverclick: Desktop application for performing mouse actions with your keyboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Neverclick：用键盘执行鼠标操作的桌面应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48877485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neverclick is a desktop tool that maps keyboard input to mouse actions, aiding accessibility and productivity for power users. It showcases how keyboard-driven navigation can complement traditional mice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Neverclick 是一个桌面实用工具，通过键盘控制鼠标，方便无鼠标操作、提升无障碍使用与生产力。它适用于需要精准定位的场景，如编程和游戏娱乐。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/45c15ce1c9850da848d5ccbc67e48de065688e0260b12540c8c3a8a2d1b1f41f/LazoVelko/neverclick' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/45c15ce1c9850da848d5ccbc67e48de065688e0260b12540c8c3a8a2d1b1f41f/LazoVelko/neverclick' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48954175' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://moonstone.sh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moonstone: Modern, cross-platform Lua runtime and package manager written in Zig</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Moonstone：用 Zig 编写的现代跨平台 Lua 运行时与包管理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48954175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48954175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Moonstone is a modern, cross-platform Lua runtime and package manager written in Zig, aiming for fast startup and portability across desktop and embedded contexts. It targets developers who want a lean, embeddable Lua stack with robust tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Moonstone 是用 Zig 编写的现代跨平台 Lua 运行时与包管理器，目标是快速启动和在桌面及嵌入式场景中的可移植性，面向寻求简洁且可嵌入的 Lua 堆栈及完善工具链的开发者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://moonstone.sh/moon.svg' data-full='https://moonstone.sh/moon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48940603' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ring-Zero: Scaling Zero RL to a Trillion Parameters for Emergent Reasoning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ring-Zero：将万亿参数用于涌现式推理的零样本强化学习</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48940603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48940603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ring-Zero scales zero-shot RL to trillion-parameter levels to study emergent reasoning capabilities, addressing training and infrastructure challenges while outlining implications for future AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ring-Zero 将零样本强化学习扩展至万亿参数规模，探索涌现式推理能力，同时讨论训练难点、计算资源和对未来AI系统的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48917755' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tucan9389/nanochat-jax/discussions/1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Porting nanochat to a TPU: what carries over from PyTorch, and what breaks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 NanoChat 移植到 TPU：哪些来自 PyTorch 的特性可用，哪些会出错</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48917755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Porting a neural chat model to TPUs reveals where PyTorch workflows map cleanly to accelerators and where custom code breaks due to XLA constraints. The effort highlights the friction between dynamic PyTorch models and the static, compiled nature of TPU backends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将一个神经聊天模型移植到 TPU 的过程揭示了哪些 PyTorch 的工作流能平滑映射到加速器、哪些在 XLA 的约束下会出错。该工作凸显了动态的 PyTorch 模型与 TPU 后端的静态编译特性之间的摩擦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/357b06b101fca4362d9756b6c71371ef5daec233368b5e30ee70178c30a6410a/tucan9389/nanochat-jax/discussions/1' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/357b06b101fca4362d9756b6c71371ef5daec233368b5e30ee70178c30a6410a/tucan9389/nanochat-jax/discussions/1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48964215' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vercel-labs/deepsec' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deepsec</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSec 安全大会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSec is a security conference; the repository collects talks, materials, and potentially talks on threat intelligence, security research, and defense techniques. It serves as a hub for researchers to share findings and for practitioners to stay updated on emerging attack surfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSec 是一场安全大会，该仓库汇集了演讲、材料和研究成果，涵盖威胁情报、漏洞研究与防御技术等主题。它为研究者分享发现、从业者了解新兴攻击面提供了一个集中的资源入口。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b4232ca640901c14b106ce2ece29ac01e3d3813041f9953eb4dc29f4af7a4634/vercel-labs/deepsec' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/b4232ca640901c14b106ce2ece29ac01e3d3813041f9953eb4dc29f4af7a4634/vercel-labs/deepsec' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48883415' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(408) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://incompleteideas.net/IncIdeas/OneStepTrap.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The One-Step Trap (In AI Research)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 研究中的“一步陷阱”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument that AI research often falls into single-step thinking, encouraging broader methodological rigor and multi-step evaluation to avoid oversimplified conclusions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章警示 AI 研究中常见的一步式思考陷阱，主张采用更全面的方法论与多阶段评估，以避免过于简单的结论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48905667' data-tags='Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.pasteurlabs.ai/projects/tesseract-core/latest/blog/2026-07-09-enzyme-lfortran-autodiff.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Differentiable Fortran with LFortran and Enzyme</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 LFortran 与 Enzyme 实现可微分 Fortran</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48905667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Combining LFortran with Enzyme enables differentiable Fortran, bringing autodiff to legacy scientific code and easing integration with ML workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 LFortran 与 Enzyme 结合，实现在 Fortran 中的可微分性，为遗留科学代码引入自动微分，并便于与 ML 工作负载整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://docs.pasteurlabs.ai/projects/tesseract-core/latest/_images/social_previews/summary_blog_2026-07-09-enzyme-lfortran-autodiff_c24bbe55.png' data-full='https://docs.pasteurlabs.ai/projects/tesseract-core/latest/_images/social_previews/summary_blog_2026-07-09-enzyme-lfortran-autodiff_c24bbe55.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48919874' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.schweizerbart.de/papers/zdgg/detail/prepub/108503/Geological_and_geophysical_characterisation_of_the_exploration_boreholes_EB1_and_EB2_Inde_Syncline_Rhenohercynian_Fold_and_Thrust_Belt_and_Weisweiler_Horst_Lower_Rhine_Embayment_Germany' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Germany maybe found a new source of renewable energy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国也许找到了新的可再生能源来源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A geological and geophysical study hints at a new renewable energy source in Germany&#x27;s Inde Syncline region. While early, such findings can influence energy diversification and policy, potentially adding to the grid mix.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国因新地质和地球物理研究在 Inde 线群区域发现潜在可再生能源来源。尽管初步，但这一发现可能影响能源多元化与政策，丰富电网结构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content5.schweizerbart.de//thumbnail/papers/108503.pdf/1000.jpg' data-full='https://content5.schweizerbart.de//thumbnail/papers/108503.pdf/1000.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48927900' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pairdrop.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>P2P local file transfer based on WebRTC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于 WebRTC 的点对点本地文件传输</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48927900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48927900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PairDrop enables P2P local file transfer using WebRTC, enabling private, server-free sharing between nearby devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PairDrop 通过 WebRTC 实现点对点本地文件传输，支持近距离设备之间的私密、无服务器共享。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fitelson.org/seminar/dawid.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The well-calibrated Bayesian [pdf] (1982)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>校准良好的贝叶斯方法 [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48921121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48921121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This PDF presents a rigorous take on Bayesian calibration, focusing on how priors shape posteriors and how to assess predictive calibration. It offers practical guidelines for building well-calibrated probabilistic models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讲解贝叶斯校准的要点，聚焦先验如何影响后验以及如何评估预测分布的校准性。并提供构建良好校准概率模型的实践要点。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 风格的并发在 C 语言中的实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go-Flavored Concurrency in C advocates for Go-style patterns in C, using channels and lightweight concurrency without giving up C&#x27;s performance. The guide shows practical patterns for structuring parallel code in C.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 风格的并发在 C 语言中的实现指南，展示如何借助通道、协程等模式在保持 C 性能的同时实现轻量级并发。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mass-driver.com/article/from-human-hands' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We don&#x27;t use AI in any of our design or production processes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mass Driver 表示在设计与生产流程中不使用 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48927373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48927373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mass Driver states it does not use AI in any design or production processes, citing reasons likely related to privacy, control, or reliability. The stance prompts reflection on when AI is appropriate and how to balance human oversight with automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mass Driver 表示在设计和生产流程中不使用 AI，理由可能涉及隐私、可控性或可靠性。此立场促使人们思考在何种场景应使用 AI，以及如何在人工监督与自动化之间取得平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A survey of unsolved problems in MLOps, from reproducibility and data quality to monitoring and evolving models. Understanding these gaps helps teams prioritize tooling, processes, and governance to scale AI responsibly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 MLOps 领域尚未解决的问题进行梳理，涵盖可复现性、数据质量、监控等方面。了解这些空白有助于团队在工具、流程和治理上做出优先级判断，以负责任地扩展 AI。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48934046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48934046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coasty.ai/docs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Coasty (YC S26) – An API for computer-use agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN：Coasty（YC S26）——用于计算机使用代理的 API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48922706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Coasty bills itself as an API for building computer-use agents, enabling automated tasks and workflows with minimal integration.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(445) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/07/19/data-center-eminent-domain-public-use/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Power companies are using eminent domain to seize land for data centers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电力公司濒用征收权为数据中心征地</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48974292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Utilities are using eminent domain to seize land for data centers, framing it as public-use infrastructure while stirring concern among landowners. The trend spotlights tensions between expanding digital infrastructure and protecting local rights, with financial and environmental implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公用事业公司正在动用征收权为数据中心征地，宣称属于公共用途基础设施，同时引发土地所有者的担忧。这一趋势凸显数字基础设施扩张与地方权利保护之间的紧张关系，并带来财政与环境方面的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48851069' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://malisper.me/the-four-horsemen-behind-thousands-of-postgres-outages/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The four horsemen behind Postgres outages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 停运背后的四大因素</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48851069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48851069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Breaks down four common culprits behind Postgres outages, including configuration pitfalls, operator errors, traffic spikes, and buggy extensions. The piece emphasizes robust operational practices and monitoring to prevent outages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分解导致 PostgreSQL 故障的四大常见原因，包括配置陷阱、运维错误、流量高峰和插件/扩展的缺陷。强调通过稳健的运维与监控来降低中断风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/7b1393790950a35251b955bc4a9188abea36408d05b7bd67b424c77a2e77092e?s=64&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/7b1393790950a35251b955bc4a9188abea36408d05b7bd67b424c77a2e77092e?s=64&amp;d=mm&amp;r=g' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>意外的匿名性</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.ubiresearchnet.com/multi-primary-color-display-technology-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Multi-Primary Color Display Emerges as Next-Gen Color Reproduction Technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多主色显示：下一代颜色重现技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48946895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Multi-primary color displays promise broader color gamuts by using more than RGB primaries, enabling richer and more accurate color reproduction. The tech faces calibration, energy and content ecosystem challenges but could redefine display quality for media and gaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多主色显示通过使用不止 RGB 的原色来扩展色域，带来更丰富、准确的色彩再现。虽然在标定、能耗和内容生态等方面存在挑战，但有望改变媒体与游戏的显示质量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebsite.pw/w/20260708-badstdcxx.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neither GCC nor Clang are compliant with standard C++</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48970039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48970039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that neither GCC nor Clang fully conforms to the C++ standard, highlighting gaps and ongoing divergence. The takeaway is to test across compilers and rely on portable, standard-conforming code where possible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张 GCC 与 Clang 都未能完全遵循 C++ 标准，指出实现中的差距与分歧。要点是对跨编译器测试保持警惕，并尽量编写可移植、符合标准的代码。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://blog.vanillajava.blog/2026/06/why-you-should-tun-code-before-your.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tune Code Before Your Garbage Collector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在垃圾回收器之前对代码进行调优</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48828983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48828983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tune Code Before Your Garbage Collector argues for optimizing allocations and object lifetimes before GC runs to improve latency. It offers practical tips for GC-backed languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张在垃圾回收器启用前优化内存分配与对象生命周期，以提升延迟和吞吐量，并给出面向 GC 的实用优化建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pocketjs.dev/blog/shipping-openstrike/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shipping OpenStrike: A Counter-Strike-Shaped FPS on a 2004 Handheld</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenStrike：在一台 2004 年代掌机上推出的 CS 风格 FPS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48874347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenStrike brings a Counter-Strike-like FPS to a 2004-era handheld, showcasing how modern game design can run on constrained hardware. The project navigates memory limits, input handling, and networking on vintage hardware, illustrating retro hardware&#x27;s enduring appeal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenStrike 将一款类似 Counter-Strike 的第一人称射击游戏移植到一台 2004 年代的掌机，展示了在受限硬件上实现现代游戏设计的可能。该项目克服了内存、输入和网络等约束，体现了复古硬件的持久魅力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48947184' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,AI,Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/PennyLaneAI/pennylane' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PennyLane is an open-source quantum software platform for quantum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PennyLane：开源量子软件平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PennyLane enables differentiable programming across quantum hardware and simulators, democratizing access to quantum machine learning and variational algorithms. Its open-source nature and framework integrations lower barriers to experimenting with quantum ML.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PennyLane 提供可在量子硬件与模拟器之间进行可微分编程的框架，使量子机器学习和变分算法更易接触。作为开源项目，良好的生态集成降低了入门与试验的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/129936360/29563eb2-8d6a-487e-aaf6-6288fcc730e2' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/129936360/29563eb2-8d6a-487e-aaf6-6288fcc730e2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Flash-MSA，利用稀疏注意力核加速百万级令牌模型的训练，显著降低显存并支持更长的上下文。讨论性能提升及对大规模 Transformer 的实际影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Counting ArXiv Delays 分析 arXiv 的提交到呈现之间的时延，揭示科研论文在获取与传播过程中的瓶颈，对依赖快速获取信息的研究者尤为重要。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://airdows.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a browser-based P2P file transfer tool using WebRTC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用浏览器实现的基于 WebRTC 的点对点文件传输工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Built a browser-based P2P file transfer tool using WebRTC to enable direct, serverless file sharing between peers. The project tackles NAT traversal, browser security, and user experience trade-offs, showing how WebRTC can enable practical, private transfers without backend infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该浏览器端点对端文件传输工具基于 WebRTC 实现，允许对等方直接在浏览器中传输文件，避免依赖服务端。实现过程涉及 NAT 穿透、浏览器安全与用户体验权衡，展示了 WebRTC 在隐私友好传输中的实际应用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agentty is a compact code-completion assistant, designed as a drop-in alternative to Claude-code, implemented in C++26 with a tiny binary footprint. It trades space for potential performance and ease of integration for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agentty 是一个体积小巧、可替代 Claude-code 的代码助手，使用 C++26 实现，二进制仅约11MB。讨论其性能、集成简便性及对开发流程的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48904912' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,Web,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rejourneyco/rejourney' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Rejourney – Open-source revenue leak prediction for web and mobile apps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Rejourney 開源的網頁/移動應用營收流失預測工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48904912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48904912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rejourney is an open-source tool that predicts revenue leakage for web and mobile apps, helping teams pinpoint where money slips away in funnels. Its open-source nature invites community contributions and rapid iteration on monetization models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rejourney 是一個開源工具，用於預測網頁與行動應用的營收流失，協助團隊找出漏失收入的環節。其開源特性促使社群貢獻與快速迭代在變現模型上的改進。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://supabase.com/blog/searchable-field-level-encryption-with-cipherstash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Searchable field-level encryption on Supabase with CipherStash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Supabase 的字段级可搜索加密（CipherStash）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48920328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48920328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CipherStash enables field-level searchable encryption, and this guide demonstrates integrating it with Supabase to keep sensitive fields encrypted while still supporting indexed search. The approach highlights how serverless stacks can offer usable privacy features without sacrificing developer productivity or performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CipherStash 提供字段级的可搜索加密，本篇演示如何把它集成到 Supabase，从而在保持敏感字段加密的同时，仍能进行索引搜索。该方案展示了无服务器架构下的隐私保护能力，以及在不牺牲开发效率和性能的前提下实现可搜索加密。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(483) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/gruns/icecream' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IceCream – Never use print() to debug again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IceCream：再也不用 print() 调试了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48964243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IceCream provides a lightweight debugging helper for Python that prints values with context using ic(), offering nicer output than plain print statements. It supports nested data structures and stack trace hints, making ad-hoc debugging more efficient.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IceCream 是一个 Python 调试工具，使用 ic() 打印变量及上下文信息，比普通 print 输出更易读。它支持嵌套数据和栈信息，能提升随手调试的效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/97221bcc4400c31fd6a1d81690f79aead0ce9b9fddd0defccb02cd7b81210f08/gruns/icecream' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/97221bcc4400c31fd6a1d81690f79aead0ce9b9fddd0defccb02cd7b81210f08/gruns/icecream' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://MasterGodzilla.github.io/posts/2025/07/muon-clip/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Muon to Gradient Clipping: Some Thoughts on QK Stability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Muon 到梯度裁剪：关于 QK 稳定性的一些思考</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48893346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the stability of QK attention patterns, touching on how numerical issues can arise during training. The piece argues that careful handling of gradients and clipping strategies can influence convergence and model reliability in large-scale attention models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨了 QK 注意力模式的稳定性，讨论训练过程中可能出现的数值问题。文章指出对梯度和裁剪策略的细致处理会影响收敛性和大型注意力模型的可靠性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In 1939, RCA Victor marketed Sound Service to schools, illustrating early educational tech marketing and the ways classroom audio systems were integrated into curricula.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1939 年，RCA Victor 将 Sound Service 推向学校市场，揭示早期教育科技的营销策略及课堂音响系统与课程整合的尝试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48867701' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(486) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mediawiki.org/wiki/Parsoid' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parsoid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Parsoid：维基媒体的解析层</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48867701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48867701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parsoid is Wikimedia&#x27;s service that converts between HTML and Wikitext to power the VisualEditor and mobile rendering. It acts as the parsing layer that ensures consistent editing experiences and page rendering, often behind the scenes in MediaWiki ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parsoid 是维基媒体用来在 HTML 与维基文本之间转换的解析服务，支撑 VisualEditor 与移动端渲染。它充当 MediaWiki 生态中稳定的解析层，确保编辑与显示的一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Parsoid_HTML-RDFa_content_model.svg/1280px-Parsoid_HTML-RDFa_content_model.svg.png?utm_source=www.mediawiki.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Parsoid_HTML-RDFa_content_model.svg/1280px-Parsoid_HTML-RDFa_content_model.svg.png?utm_source=www.mediawiki.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bartoszmilewski.com/2026/07/14/tannakian-reconstruction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tannakian Reconstruction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tannakian 重构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48907263' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accessible tour of Tannakian reconstruction, a concept linking algebraic groups with tensor categories in modern representation theory. The post builds intuition and hints at implications for how symmetry and categories organize algebraic data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Tannakian 重构的直观讲解，将代数群与张量范畴在现代表示论中的联系讲解清楚，帮助理解对称性与范畴如何组织代数数据及其应用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lingbot-map introduces a 3D foundation model that reconstructs scenes from streaming data, enabling real-time mapping for robotics, AR/VR, and digital twins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lingbot-map 引入一个从流数据重建场景的 3D 基础模型，支持实时三维映射，适用于机器人、AR/VR 与数字孪生等场景。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48953519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Documenting an interaction where Claude Code refused to slow down when asked, illustrating model constraints on pacing and responsiveness. The incident highlights safety or UX considerations around control of AI behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>记录了一次与 Claude Code（Fable）的互动：在被要求放慢速度时，模型拒绝执行，揭示了对节奏控制的约束。此事揭示了在 AI 响应速度与安全策略之间的权衡，以及对用户体验的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Aurora DSQL：可扩展的多区域 OLTP</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aurora DSQL presents a scalable, multi-region OLTP system, enabling distributed transactions across geographies. The work surveys architecture and performance implications for global databases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aurora DSQL 提供一个可扩展的多区域在线事务处理系统，支持跨地区分布式事务。文章评估了全球数据库的架构与性能影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interdisciplinary piece ties the Fermi Paradox to percolation theory and possible implications for genetic or societal spread, offering a fresh mathematical lens on civilization&#x27;s fate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇跨学科文章将费米悖论与渗流理论联系起来，探讨可能影响文明命运的扩散与遗传因素，提供了新的数学视角。</p>
